2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 美國 9 月 ISM 非製造業 PMI 從 8 月的 55.4 降至 54.9，顯示服務業活動增速放
緩，然而國內需求強勁使供應鏈承壓，並推動投入價格指數由 72.6 升至 74.0 為
四年多來新高；另一方面，美國 10 月密西根大學消費者信心指數由 9 月的 48.1
降至 46.3，為連續第三個月下降且創 5 月以來最低 ， 惟未來一年通膨預期由 4.6%
升至 4.7%，顯示通膨可能持續維持高位至 2027 年。
⚫ 聯準會 9 月 FOMC 會議紀要顯示與會官員均支持升息，並認為年底前尚需升息
一次 ，但對於後續升息時點及升息理由上存在分歧； 其中部分官員認為升息是防
止能源及其他價格衝擊造成更廣泛影響 的預防性措施，但更為鷹派官員則認為
升息在於抑制投資和支出，以防範正在顯現的需求驅動型通膨。
⚫ 歐洲央行 9 月貨幣政策會議紀要顯示全體決策委員均同意升息，並認為當前經
濟成長風險偏向下行 ， 通膨風險則偏向上行 ，因此將保留政策彈性， 根據經濟數

據做為決策，不提供未來利率路徑指引。
⚫ 台灣 9 月 CPI 年增率自 8 月 2.05%上升至 2.73%，已連續 5 個月超過 2%通膨警
戒線 ， 且創兩年半來最大漲幅 ，除了豪雨導致蔬果價格上漲及中秋假期的短期因
素外，油價居高不下亦使通膨壓力增加。
⚫ 中國央行發布匯率政策立場聲明， 強調中國貿易發展根源於產業國際競爭力的
提升，無必要通過匯率貶值獲取貿易競爭優勢 ，並指出中國長期實施以市場供需
為基礎、參考一籃子貨幣進行調節管理浮動匯率制度。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Oct/12/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.699，低點在 31.940，以 31.875 作收，較前一週收盤價
貶值 4.5 分。 
上週台幣震盪走貶，週初，受到前週公布的美國非農就業數據低於預期，降低
市場對聯準會 10 月升息的預期， 台股創高吸引外資匯入 ， 推升台幣最高來到31.699；
不過隨著美伊緊張情勢升溫且台灣連假將至 ， 台股收低使外資轉為大幅賣超並匯出 ，
推低台幣回到 9 月中以來的震盪區間下緣，最低觸及 31.940，最終在央行調節之下
收在 31.875。 
＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣區間震盪。就國際市場而言，美國 10 月密西根大學消費者信心
指數持續下滑，由 9 月的 48.1 降至 46.3，創 5 月以來最低，惟未來一年通膨預期
升至 4.7%，加上中東局勢反覆，油價高位震盪，仍支撐市場對於聯準會年內升息
的預期，預計美元指數維持在 102 附近震盪，對台幣帶來壓力；就台灣市場來看，
台灣 9 月出口創歷史新高，年增 60.9%，反映 AI 及科技需求持續強勁，出口商後
續拋匯需求可望提供台幣支撐；然而，台股逼近五萬點關卡之際，外資未能延續買
超走勢，且連假前已大幅賣超台股，若後續外資持續匯出，將限制台幣升值空間。
綜合以上，預估本週台幣區間震盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.700-32.000

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Oct/12/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣區間震盪 。 中國十一長假期間 (10/1~10/7) 境內人民幣市場休市 ， 而
境外人民幣維持偏強格局 ， 主要在6.7000 上方震盪 ， 臨近周末 ， 人行中間價為6.7330，
創 3 年多來新高，加上美元指數漲勢放緩，推升境外人民幣突破 6.7000 整數關卡，
終場境外人民幣收在 6.6927，週線升值 134 點，境內人民幣收在 6.6929，較十一長
假前夕升值 121 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週人民幣區間偏升。就國際市場來看，美國密西根大學消費者信心指數
由 9 月的 48.1 降至 46.3，創5 月以來新低 ， 不過對於未來一年的通膨預期升至4.7%，
加上油價維持高檔 ， 市場對於聯準會年內升息的預期仍存 ， 美元指數維持高檔震盪 ，
將限制人民幣升值速度；就中國國內市場來看，人行中間價維持偏強設定，不過人
行表示將防範短期大幅波動與急貶風險 ， 並無意透過人民幣貶值取得貿易競爭優勢 ，
有助於穩定市場對人民幣的預期 ；最後就技術面來看 ，USD/CNH 日線圖中 ， 各天期
均線仍向下發散，空頭格局不變，下方支撐先看向 9 月底低點 6.6908。綜合以上，
預估本週人民幣區間偏升。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6800-6.7100

### Page 5

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Oct/12/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY 震盪走升。週初，因美元指數在102 附近盤整，帶領USD/JPY 延
續前一週走勢震盪走升到 158 上方；週間，因缺乏重要經濟數據，市場偏向觀望，
USD/JPY 維持在 158 附近震盪；臨近週末，USD/JPY 曾一度回測 157.50 附近，不
過在美日利差的主導下，USD/JPY 依舊保持偏多的走勢，終場收在 158.31，週線上
漲 84 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、路透短觀調查顯示，受半導
體強勁訂單推動，日本 10 月大型製造業信心升至正 22，創近五年新高；但原料成
本高漲重擊非製造業，信心指數降至正 23。與此同時，此前投下升息反對票的日銀
鴿派委員佐藤綾野立場軟化，表態支持分階段升息，市場對後續正常化預期升溫，
預計將限縮 USD/JPY 的上行空間 。二 、 日本首相高市早苗表示密切關注匯率與物價
走勢 ， 強調日本已脫離通縮 ， 將完全尊重央行政策獨立性 ， 暗示不反對進一步升息 。
面對通膨加劇與日圓走貶壓力，其過往堅持的貨幣寬鬆立場已出現轉向。然而目前
美日實質利差仍大，持續為 USD/JPY 提供支撐。三、就技術面而言，日線圖中，
USD/JPY 持續沿著上彎的月線上攻，然匯價持續承壓於季線下方，在有效站上季線
及突破前高壓力前，仍以區間震盪看待。綜合以上，預估USD/JPY 本週呈現區間震
盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：157.10-159.50。 
前週交易區間 
157.41 158.50

### Page 6

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Oct/12/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 震盪 走跌 。週初， 因法國債券風險與區域政治不確定性 ，
EUR/USD 下行最低來到 1.1160； 週間 ，受中東地緣風險升溫與歐洲財政疑慮帶動避
險需求 ， 加上美債殖利率維持高檔 ， 美元利差優勢延續，歐元維持低檔； 臨近週末 ，
逢低歐元有買盤支撐，帶動歐元收復部分跌幅，終場收在 1.1201，週線下跌 46 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 區間偏空震盪，主要原因如下：一、歐洲央行首席經濟學
家連恩表示，夏末能源成本飆升為經濟增長帶來下行風險，並可能限制央行收緊貨
幣政策的力道，與此同時，歐元區 10 月 Sentix 投資者信心指數由 5.1 降至 2.7，德
國預期指數同步滑落，反映市場對經濟復甦的信心明顯降溫，預計將令歐元持續承
壓。二、法國社會動盪與支出訴求阻礙財政減赤進程，加劇債務可持續性疑慮，並
推升法德公債利差與信用風險，令歐元承壓走弱。相較美國經濟展現韌性且聯準會
維持偏鷹立場，歐洲深陷景氣放緩與財政掣肘，歐洲央行緊縮空間受限。在歐美基
本面與政策前景持續分歧下 ，EUR/USD 缺乏實質支撐 ， 後市將維持偏弱整理並下探
關鍵支撐。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 匯價雖多次向下測試 1.1165 關
鍵價位均獲得支撐，惟每次反彈均無法站上五日線，且各天期均線下彎呈現空頭排
列。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD 本週呈現區間偏空震盪的可能性較高。

＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1100-1.1260 
前週交易區間 
1.1160 1.1276

7  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115.10.05-115.10.08 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.830%  
30 天期短票利率   1.66% ~ 1.68% 
公債－10 年期-A15109     2.05% 
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.82%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分 ， 本週邁入新的提存期 ， 央行連日減發存單 ， 令市場資金供
給充裕，帶動短率朝區間低位移動。 
週一央行標售 364 天期定存單 1100 億元 ， 加權平均得標利率為1.602%， 低於
市場預期區間 1.61%-1.635%，較上期標售結果下跌 4.1 個基本點。 
週 四 隔 夜 拆 款 利 率 區 間 在 0.805%~0.830% ， 30 天 期 短 票 主 要 成 交 在
1.67%~1.68%，利率大致持平於上週。 
二、 受美債利率續創新高影響，週四 10 年期指標券 A15109 成交在 2.05%，創同
年期券自 2008 年以來高點，5 年期指標券 A15108 則未成交，最近成交利率
為 1.82%。 
台灣 9 月 CPI 年增率自 8 月 2.05%上升至 2.73%，已連續 5 個月超過 2%通膨
警戒線 ， 且創兩年半來最大漲幅 ， 目前油價仍居高難下 ， 通膨壓力將令央行維
持偏緊的貨幣政策，加上國際債市利率持續走升，台債殖利率恐面臨上行壓
力，預估 5 年券區間為 1.85%~1.95%，10 年券區間為 2.0%~2.10%。

## Document 061: 2026年9月通胀数据点评：生产与消费价格修复分化，内需回暖尚有“时间差”

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年9月通胀数据点评：生产与消费价格修复分化，内需回暖尚有“时间差”.pdf
Report source: 西南证券
Date: 2026-10-15
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

请务必阅读正文后的重要声明部分  
1 
[Table_ReportInfo] 2026 年 10 月 14 日      
证 券研究报告 •宏观简评报告      
数据点评  
生产与消费 价格修复分化 ，内需 回暖 尚有 “时间差 ” 
——2026 年 9 月通胀数据点评

点评

西 南证券研究院  
[Table_Summary]  摘要：CPI方面，9 月 CPI 同比上涨 0.9%，涨幅较上月扩大0.1个百分点，连
续两个月回升，但低于市场预期。食品价格同比降幅扩大至1.7%，主要受鲜
菜、 鲜果价格下跌影响， 猪价同比跌幅收窄； 非食品价格同比涨幅扩大至1.5%，
主要受国际油价反弹及电子产品价格上涨带动，其中汽油价格同比上涨
16.6%，拉动整体 CPI同比上涨约 0.47 个百分点。环比看，CPI 上涨 0.2%，
涨幅较上月收窄 0.2 个百分点，核心 CPI 同比涨幅持平于 1.0%，服务价格环
比转跌，反映国内终端需求修复仍偏温和。展望10 月，国际油价高位运行及
AI 算力需求带动的消费电子涨价仍将提供支撑， 但蔬果价格同比降幅扩大、 猪
肉价格环比转跌，叠加服务价格季节性回落，CPI 整体或延续温和修复。PPI
方面，9 月 PPI 同比上涨 5.0%， 涨幅较上月扩大1.2个百分点， 高于市场预期，
主要受国际油价反弹及低基数效应推动。分行业看，煤炭开采和洗选业、 石油
和天然气开采业、石油煤炭及其他燃料加工业价格同比涨幅分别扩大至
35.3%、24.5%和 21.6%，化工、化纤等行业价格亦明显回升，反映能源价格
上涨正沿产业链向中游原材料环节传导；电气机械、 计算机通信及其他电子设
备制造业价格环比均上涨0.4%，AI 算力相关需求形成一定支撑。 环比看，PPI
上涨 1.2%，涨幅较上月扩大 0.8 个百分点，石油相关行业价格上涨是主要拉
动。展望后续，美伊围绕霍尔木兹海峡的紧张局势、国际油价高位运行、冬季
煤炭备货及低基数效应仍将支撑PPI， 但上下游价格传导不畅、 终端消费偏弱，
生活资料价格上涨空间仍然有限，PPI 修复或延续结构性分化。 
 输入性因素支撑 CPI 同比修复，核心 CPI 保持稳定。同比来看，9 月 CPI 同
比涨幅扩大 0.1个百分点至 0.9%，连续两个月回升，增速低于wind一致预期
（1.02%）。其中，食品价格同比下降1.7%，降幅扩大 0.3个百分点。非食品
价格同比涨幅扩大 0.3 个百分点至 1.5%，主要受能源及电子产品价格涨幅扩
大影响。9 月中东地缘冲突持续发酵， 能源供应风险上升， 国际油价二次反弹，
带动国内能源价格涨幅扩大，对整体CPI 形成支撑。环比来看，9 月 CPI环比
涨幅收窄 0.2个百分点至 0.2%， 其中食品价格环比上升 0.4%，持平上月表现，
非食品价格环比涨幅收窄 0.1 个百分点至 0.2%。9 月核心 CPI 同比涨幅持平
于 1%， 核心CPI 环比回落至 0%。核心 CPI 连续数月运行在 1.0%附近， 说明
剔除输入性扰动后，国内终端需求的修复力度仍然温和。展望 10 月，国际油
价高位运行及 AI算力需求带动的消费电子价格上涨仍将为CPI提供支撑，但
食品价格仍面临一定下行压力。 同时， 暑期结束后服务价格进入季节性回落阶
段，核心 CPI 修复力度预计仍偏温和。整体来看，输入性因素仍将对CPI 形
成支撑，但内需修复偏缓或限制涨幅，预计10 月 CPI 同比延续温和回升。 
 食品价格同比降幅扩大，蔬果价格下跌是主要拖累。同比来看，9 月食品价格
同比下降 1.7%，降幅扩大 0.3 个百分点，主要受鲜菜、鲜果价格下跌及猪肉
价格仍处低位影响。 食品细项中，鲜菜、鲜果价格同比分别下降8.1%、1.5%，
降幅均扩大，反映季节性供给增加对农产品价格形成压制。牛肉、 羊肉价格同
比分别上升 7.5%、6.4%，涨幅均扩大。猪价同比下降 10.0%，降幅收窄 1.8
个百分点，价格弱势有所缓解，但同比仍处于较大跌幅区间。环比来看，食品
价格环比上升 0.4%，持平上月表现。食品细项中，鲜菜价格环比涨幅收窄5
个百分点至 0.5%，鲜果价格环比由下降转为上升 0.6%。猪价环比涨幅收窄
0.1 个百分点至 1.2%，9 月猪肉均价同比下降 13.58%，降幅继续收窄。从最
新的高频数据走势看，截至 2026年 10 月 13 日，同比方面，10 月初 28 种重
点监测蔬菜价格同比下降 17.1%，7 种重点监测水果价格同比下降 6.1%，同

比降幅均扩大。鸡蛋价格同比涨幅收窄至31.7%，猪肉价格同比下降 11.5%，
降幅收窄；环比方面，28 种重点监测蔬菜价格环比由上升转为下降5.9%，7
种重点监测水果价格环比下降 0.3%，降幅收窄。鸡蛋价格环比由上升转为下
降 9.6%，猪肉价格环比由上升转为下降2.2%。结合 10 月初高频数据，蔬果
价格同比降幅进一步扩大， 猪肉价格环比转跌， 食品价格短期仍面临一定下行
压力，猪肉价格修复尚不稳定，后续仍需观察生猪出栏节奏、养殖端产能调整
及消费需求变化。
 能源和电子产品支撑非食品价格， 暑期结束拖累服务价格环比表现。同比来看，
9 月非食品价格同比涨幅扩大0.3个百分点至 1.5%，主要受国际油价回升带动
能源价格上涨影响。9 月汽油价格同比涨幅扩大 7.3 个百分点至 16.6%，仅此
一项即影响 CPI同比上涨约 0.47 个百分点， 带动整体能源价格涨幅从4.1%跃
升至 7.3%。扣除能源的工业消费品价格上涨1.6%，涨幅回落 0.2 个百分点。

其中黄金饰品价格上涨 22.5%，涨幅回落 11.1 个百分点；数据存储设备、平
板电脑、 计算机和移动电话机价格分别上涨46.7%、23.6%、21.8%和 14.9%，
涨幅均有扩大，反映电子产品价格仍有较强支撑。服务价格同比上升 0.8%，
涨幅持平上月。环比来看，9 月非食品价格环比涨幅收窄0.1个百分点至 0.2%。
工业消费品价格上涨 0.7%，涨幅比上月回落 0.1 个百分点，其中国内汽油价
格上涨 4.9%，涨幅比上月回落2.3 个百分点， 黄金饰品价格由上月上涨7.6%
转为下降 2.1%。服务 CPI 环比由上升转为下降 0.2%。服务中， 随着暑期结束，
飞机票、 宾馆住宿和旅行社收费价格分别下降8.7%、7.0%和 6.0%； 受成本走
高影响，移动电话机、计算机、数据存储设备和平板电脑价格分别上涨 3.7%、
2.0%、1.7%和 1.5%。展望 10 月，非食品价格预计延续分化走势，同比仍受
到能源价格回升和电子产品涨价支撑， 但环比上涨动能可能进一步减弱。 一方

面， 国际油价同比改善以及电子产品价格上涨仍将支撑非食品价格表现； 另一
方面， 暑期结束后服务价格进入季节性回落阶段， 叠加黄金等前期涨价品类涨
幅收窄，非食品价格环比或继续放缓。
 油价反弹带动 PPI超预期回升，资源品价格韧性较强。从同比看，受国际油价
涨幅回升及低基数效应影响，PPI同比涨幅扩大 1.2 个百分点至 5.0%，增速高
于 wind 一致预期 （4.3%）。生产资料同比涨幅扩大1.5 个百分点至 6.5%，生
活资料同比下降 0.1%，降幅收窄 0.4 个百分点。分行业看，煤炭开采和洗选
业同比涨幅扩大 8.7个百分点至 35.3%，主要受冬季供暖备煤需求增加、 主产
矿区供应阶段性偏紧、国际煤价上涨预期效应等因素影响。石油和天然气开采
业、石油煤炭及其他燃料加工业价格同比涨幅分别扩大14.0、10.5 个百分点
至 24.5%和 21.6%。化学纤维制造业、 化学原料及化学制品制造业同比涨幅分
别扩大至 12.3%和 12.1%。一方面， 国际原油价格上涨直接推升采掘和炼化环

节价格；另一方面，石油作为基础原材料，通过燃料、化工品等渠道向中下游
传导， 带动化学原料、 化纤等行业价格上涨。从环比看，PPI环比涨幅扩大 0.8
个百分点至 1.2%， 生产资料环比涨幅扩大 1 个百分点至 1.4%， 生活资料环比
涨幅收窄 0.1 个百分点至 0.1%。分行业看，煤炭开采和洗选业环比涨幅扩大
6.7 个百分点至 9.5%；石油相关行业价格上涨动能显著增强。 石油和天然气开
采业、 石油煤炭及其他燃料加工业价格环比涨幅分别扩大4.3、4.9个百分点至
10.4%和 8.4%。电气机械和器材制造业、计算机通信和其他电子设备制造业
价格环比均上涨 0.4%，AI 与算电协同带来的结构性需求推动资本开支扩张。
国际层面，10 月 13 日布伦特原油现货价格为130.92美元/桶，10 月初油价同
比涨幅扩大 28.36 个百分点至 100.08%。10 月以来，美伊围绕霍尔木兹海峡
的紧张局势持续升温，伊朗加强对海上运输活动限制，美国持续强化对伊朗石
油贸易的封锁，区域供应风险上升。原油市场供给担忧升温，国际油价或维持

高位，并通过能源、化工等产业链向国内工业品价格传导。9 月，PPI 同比涨
幅（5.0%）与 CPI 同比涨幅（0.9%）之间的差距达到 4.1 个百分点，工厂端
的原材料和能源成本正在快速上涨，但尚未同步传导至终端消费市场，中下游
企业的利润空间正受到挤压。后续来看，国际油价高位震荡、冬季煤炭备货叠
加低基数构成后续 PPI 的核心推升力量。石油与煤炭开采价格同比或继续走
阔，并向化工、化纤等中下游传导；AI 算力相关电气设备、通信设备制造价
格或维持增长。 但上下游传导仍不畅， 终端消费偏弱或仍制约生活资料涨价空
间。
 风险提示：国内政策落地节奏不及预期，国际大宗商品价格波动超预期， 海外
地缘扰动风险。

分析师承诺  
本报告署名分析师具有中国证券业协会授予的证券投资咨询执业资格并注册为证券分析师，报告所采用的数据均
来自合法合规 渠道，分析逻辑基于分析师的职业理解，通过合理判断得出结论，独立、客观地出具本报告。分析师承
诺不曾因，不因，也将不会因本报告中的具体推荐意见或观点而直接或间接获取任何形式的补偿。

投资评级说明  
报告中投资建议所涉及的评级分为公司评级和行业评级（另有说明的除外）。评级标准为报告发布日后 6 个月内
的相对市场表现，即：以报告发布日后 6 个月内公司股价（或行业指数）相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数的涨跌幅
作为基准。其中：A 股市场以沪深 300 指数为基准， 新三板市场以三板成指 （针对协议转让标的） 或三板做市指数 （针
对做市转让标的）为基准；香港市场以恒生指数为基准；美国市场以纳斯达克综合指数或标普 500 指数为基准。  
公司评级  
买入：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅在 20%以上  
持有：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅介于 10%与 20%之间 
中性：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅介于 -10%与 10%之间  
回避：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅介于 -20%与-10%之间  
卖出：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅在 -20%以下  
行业评级  
强于大市：未来 6 个月内，行业整体回报高于同期相关证券市场代表性指数 5%以上 
跟随大市：未来 6 个月内，行业整体回报介于同期相关证券市场代表性指数 -5%与 5%之间 
弱于大市：未来 6 个月内，行业整体回报低于同期相关证券市场代表性指数 -5%以下

Update
Largan Precision  | Asia Pacific
3Q26 Results Miss on Lower
GPM, but FAU/CPO
Development Well on Track
Largan reported 3Q26 net income of NT$5.182bn, down 27% YoY.
The company reported a 11% YoY revenue decline, to NT$15.651bn; gross margin
declined YoY and QoQ to 42.2% in 3Q26, lower than expected due to lower new-
project yields and structural green electricity cost hikes.
Management expects shipments to be flat MoM in October and decline

sequentially in November as order momentum is weaker-than-expected.
FAU/CPO development well on track: First FA  project passed customer
verification; expanding from FA into FAU, including coupling and potential FAU-to-
carrier assembly, with a dedicated new facility expected to support mass production
from around mid-2027. We maintain our long-term positive view on Largan's FAU/
CPO opportunities.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Largan Precision (3008.TW)
Base case: Our price target is based on an SOTP valuation, comprising the traditional business
and potential FAU opportunity. We value the traditional business using a residual income
model, consistent with our Greater China Hardware Tech coverage, assuming an 8.5% cost of
equity, a 7% net profit CAGR over 2026-36E and a 3% terminal growth rate. We separately

value the FAU business, assuming a 40% market share in 2028.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected high-end smartphone demand
n Faster-than-expected penetration of periscopes
n Stronger-than-expected CPO volume growth
Risks to Downside
n Greater-than-expected competition, which would erode Largan's ASP and gross margin
n Weaker-than-expected smartphone demand, especially for high-end models
n Market share gains and more pricing pressure from Sunny

n Weaker-than-expected CPO volume growth

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

ab 12 October 2026
Global Research
Nanya Technology
Further upside to expectations likely
Another strong quarter
Nanya Tech (NTC) reported strong 3Q26 revenue of NT$133.6bn (+62% QoQ,
+612% YoY) in line with UBSe of NT$132.7bn and 6% above Visible Alpha consensus
of NT$126.5bn. EPS came in at $25.40, slightly below UBSe of NT$26.44 and modestly
above consensus of NT$24.40. Growth was driven primarily by strong ASPs, up 65%
QoQ (vs UBSe of +50%) mainly on the back of the renewal of 6-month and 1-year

contracts that had expired, partially offset by lower-than-expected bit growth of -
5% QoQ (vs UBSe of +5% QoQ). Profitability, again, improved sequentially, with gross/
operating margins rising to 85.7%/80.0% from 79.5%/73.7% in 2Q26.
Fulfilment rates to remain low until Fab 5A ramp
NTC continues to see supportive supply-demand dynamics, with fulfilment rates for
demand outside of long-term contracts – which account for close to 70% of bits already

– remaining low. That  said, NTC sees limited upside to unit shipments in the near term,
given low inventory levels and limited floor space: (1) reiterating its full-year bit growth
guidance of "up high-teens % YoY," implying 4Q unit shipments will decline MSD
% QoQ (UBSe: -6%); and (2) guiding shipments to remain flat thereafter until its new
fab begins to ramp in 2H27. NTC's efforts to bring Fab 5A online are progressing well,
with equipment move-in scheduled for 1Q27 and initial production expected by 3Q27.

NTC continues to expect wafer capacity to reach 30k wpm by the end of 2028 and 50k
by the end of 2030. With demand remaining strong and existing capacity not enough to
fully address this, NTC continues to see upside to sequential ASP growth in the near
term, although at a more moderate pace.
Changes to forecasts
Reflecting 3Q26 results, as well as our new bit growth and ASP forecasts, we keep our
2026E forecasts largely unchanged but slightly raise our 2027E/2028E revenue forecasts

by 7%/5% to NT$655bn/NT$735bn (from NT$613bn/NT$702bn previously) and
EPS forecasts by 10%/7% to NT$128.16/NT$134.53 (from NT$116.41/NT$125.68
previously). Our 2027E/2028E EPS forecasts now sit 10%/8% above pre-results Visible
Alpha consensus.
Valuation: PT NT$640/share; maintain Buy
We value Nanya Tech shares at 2.63x NTM P/BV based on our 2028E-31E ROE estimate
of 21.4% and CoE of 8.1%.

### Page 2

Nanya Technology UBS ResearchNanya Tech (2408.TW)
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Will DRAM undersupply continue into 2028?
Yes, in our view. Agentic AI is boosting further memory demand on multiple fronts beyond HBM,
including DDR5/LPDDR5 for conventional servers and CPU head nodes in AI servers. We now forecast
an acceleration of memory demand growth into 2027 with DRAM bit end-consumption growing

40% YoY vs 21% in 2026. As almost all of the incremental wafer capacity addition for DRAM is
going to HBM, supply is not catching up with demand. The main risk remains affordability by
customers as memory industry revenues are expected to reach US$1.69tn in 2027E.
Q: Can NTC grow as a niche DRAM player?
Yes, we think 2027 is likely to be a year of more constrained growth. Subsequently, though, we think
its process tech migration to 1c nm and 1d nm will likely lead to a 30% increase in bits per wafer for

each process tech migration (at comparable yields). NTC is also slightly pulling in its new fab ramp,
with equipment expected to move in 1Q27. We also see a tail of DDR3 and DDR4 products NTC can
address for a while.
Nanya Tech shares trade at 2.17x NTM P/BV, above +1SD of its 10-year average. That said, we believe
the strength of the unfolding DRAM cycle, as well as the tail of legacy products that Nanya can
address as a niche player, should continue to provide support for the stock.

Our industry checks suggest HBM demand continues to result in capacity allocation away from DDR,
which may moderate possible cyclical corrections, supporting upside to DDR contract pricing for
longer.
At 2.17x NTM P/BV, we believe Nanya Tech shares are discounting a LT average ROE of 17.6% vs our
LT estimate of 21.4%.

Company Description
Nanya Tech is a pure DRAM company based in Taiwan, focusing on legacy products for
consumers, broad-based applications and is now growing in servers.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Nanya Tech is a pure-play DRAM manufacturer based in Taiwan mainly focused on legacy DDR

products for customers across various end applications. We think downside risks to our thesis
could include a potential slowdown in end demand and hence the conventional memory
cycle as well as Nanya's legacy-centric product portfolio. We value Nanya Tech shares using a
target NTM P/BV multiple based on long-term average ROE and cost of equity.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.
Important Information About Sustainable Investing Strategies: Sustainable investing strategies aim to consider and incorporate environmental, social and governance
(ESG) factors into investment process and portfolio construction. Strategies across geographies approach ESG analysis and incorporate the findings in a variety of ways.
Incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing considerations may inhibit the portfolio manager’s ability to participate in certain investment opportunities that otherwise

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

迅得為自動化設備供應商 
 迅得為自動化設備供應商 
迅得機械成立於 1999 年，主要產品為印刷電路板、軟板、光電及半導體物流系
統之自動化解決方案，2025 年營收比重可區分為 PCB 事業部(41%)、半導體-
載板事業部 13%、半導體-晶圓事業部 12%、半導體-封裝事業部 32%，面板事
業部 2%。主要客戶包括欣興、南亞、景碩、台郡、臻鼎、日月光、台積電、友
達、群創等。

載板 、 晶圓客戶 、OSAT 客戶均樂
觀，2027 年營收挑戰 100 億元

載板、晶圓客戶、OSAT 客戶均樂觀，2027 年營收挑戰 100 億元 
迅得 1H26 營收占比為 PCB 4 1%，載板 16%，晶圓製造 16%，封裝 25%，其
他 2%。PCB 主要產品以自動上下料、物件搬運及智慧倉儲為主； IC 載板亦為
自動上下料較多，整廠規畫約佔比 60%，單機搬運 20%，倉儲 15~20%；半導
體晶圓廠則以倉儲為主，台灣部分標準倉儲較多，微型倉儲較少， 海外部分目
前未出貨標準倉儲，僅供應微型倉儲，主要是安裝時間及人力配置考量；封裝
OSAT 廠主要供應倉儲佔比 60~70%，自動化搬運約 30%。

載板客戶積極擴產，載板三雄欣興、南電、景碩均為公司客戶，近年積極布局
轉戰載板市場的臻鼎亦為客戶，以欣興資本支出來看，2026 年 340 億元，2027
年長交期採購設備訂單金額 92 億元 ， 均將投入桃園楊梅廠及光復廠 ； 景碩2026
年資本支出 140 億元(原本是 80 億元)，將投入於六廠建置及設備導入，2027
年資本支出為 160 億元(原本是 100 億元)， 擴產進度明確 。 由於載板面積增大，
技術難度提升 ，為了維持相同生產效率須提高自動化資本支出 ，故以過往來看，
自動化設備佔客戶資本之約為 5%，現在提升至 15%。

晶圓製造部分，隨 客戶台灣及海外積極擴產，因目前現有自動化廠商均無單一
廠商可獨立供足客戶所需 自動化設備量體，故每個案件 公司均有相對應配比，
以海外廠來說，考量人力成本及安裝時間公司僅提供微型倉儲為主。 OSAT 部
分，客戶拉貨持續積極，半導體業務維持樂觀。以交期來看，過去 4~6 個月現
在已經拉長到 6-8 個月，載板、晶圓客戶、OSAT 客戶均樂觀，2027 年營收挑
戰 100 億元，預計 2027 年將新增產能 40%，以支應客戶未來需求。

維持買進，目標價 220 元

維持買進評等，目標價 220 元 
3Q26~2027 年營收逐季增長 ， 新產能逐步開出 ， 故維持買進 ， 預估2026 年 EPS 
8.51 元，2027 年 EPS 11.40 元(原估 10.95 元)， 目標價220 元。(2027 PER 20x)

風險提示 
出貨不如預期，ASP 不如預期。

반도체업종

◼ 최근 시장은 2가지 Risk를 제기. 다만, 과도한 우려는 기우. 
◼ 현 주가는 보다 강인해지는 Fundamental의 가치를 미반영한 수준. 
◼ 비중 확대 전략 유효. 목표주가와 매수의견 유지.

투자전략: 업황을 대변하지 못하고 있는 주가 
목표주가와 매수의견 유지. 현 주가는 업황의 강세를 충분히 반영하지 못하
고 있다고 생각. 1) 메모리반도체 사이클의 추가 확장 (구매 경쟁 심화에 따른 
단기 가격 상승 폭 확대, 기대치를 상회할 4Q26 DRAM 판매량), 2) 3Q26 실
적발표에서 보다 구체화될 주주환원 정책의 방향성에 보다 집중할 필요. 
시장에서 제기하는 Risk가 있다면, 2가지. 1) 주요 고객사향 HBM4 품질 이슈, 
2) Solidigm IPO와 해외 공장 투자에 따른 경계심리 확대가 그 대상. 당사는 
해당 Risk가 Fundamental을 저해할 것으로 보고 있지 않으며, 단기 노이즈보
다는 사이클 확장의 선순환에 보다 주목할 필요가 있다고 생각. 
1. HBM4 품질 이슈, 과도한 우려는 기우 
3Q26부터 HBM4 출하가 본격화될 것이라는 기존 입장을 유지. HBM4 비중
은 3Q26 전체 판매량의 40%, 2027년 연간 50% 중반까지 상승할 것. 
일각에서는 주요 고객사향 HBM4 품질 이슈로 사업 성과가 기대치를 하회할 
수 있다는 우려를 표출. 다만, 과도한 우려는 기우. 후공정 단에서 문제가 일
부 발생했을 수 있으나, 해결 불가능한 Risk라 보기 어렵고, 불량에 따른 배
상금을 논할 단계도 아니라는 판단. 
2. 해외 공장 투자, 실보다 득이 크다 
언론보도에 따르면, SK하이닉스는 해외 공장 투자와 Solidigm IPO를 추진 중. 
해당 결정이 현실화될 경우, 시장은 단기적으로 이를 부정적으로 받아들일 
가능성이 존재. 다만, 결과적으로, 실보다 득이 큰 선택이 될 것이라 생각. 
- 해외 투자가 현실화되더라도, 실질 생산 기여 시점은 2028년 이후일 가능
성이 높음. 미국의 건설 경기와 공장 구축의 세부 단계 등을 감안할 필요. 
- 해외 투자 결정이 관세 Risk를 축소시킬 수 있는 변화라는 점도 감안할 필
요. “불확실성 해소”는 Upcycle의 중반부에서 반도체 주식에 대한 좋은 투자
포인트가 되어왔고, 이번에도 다르지 않을 것이라는 판단.

- Overweight(비중확대) 
  : 향후 6개월간 업종지수상승률이 시장수익률 대비 초과 상승 예상

  : 향후 6개월간 업종지수상승률이 시장수익률과 유사한 수준 예상 
- Underweight(비중축소) 
  : 향후 6개월간 업종지수상승률이 시장수익률 대비 하회 예상

기업 투자의견 
- Buy(매수)  
  : 향후 6개월간 시장수익률 대비 10%p 이상 주가 상승 예상 
- Marketperform(시장수익률) 
  : 향후 6개월간 시장수익률 대비 -10%p~10%p 주가 변동 예상 
- Underperform(시장수익률 하회) 
  : 향후 6개월간 시장수익률 대비 10%p 이상 주가 하락 예상

## Document 204: [207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 단기 부담에도 컨센서스 부합 기대삼성바이오로직스(207940) 단기 부담에도 컨센서스 부합 기대단기 부담_2a8cdc97

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 단기 부담에도 컨센서스 부합 기대삼성바이오로직스(207940) 단기 부담에도 컨센서스 부합 기대단기 부담_2a8cdc97.pdf

(26.10.02) 
1,354,000 
제약업종

◼ 3Q26 컨센서스 부합, 4Q26 성장폭 확대 전망 
◼ 5공장 Ramp-up 지속, 신규 수주 회복 여부에 주목 
◼ 투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지 
목표주가는 DCF 방식으로 산출(WACC 8.1%, 영구성장률 3.5% 적용)

3Q26 Preview: 단기 부담에도 컨센서스 부합 전망 
3Q26 매출액 1조 3,380억원(+6.4% yoy, +1.3% qoq), 영업이익 5,890억원(-
7.0% yoy, +0.4% qoq, OPM 44.0%)으로 컨센서스에 부합하는 실적을 전
망함. 3분기 평균 원/달러 환율이 전분기 대비 약 5% 하락하고, 지난 5월 
파업에 따른 생산 차질 영향이 반영되나, 5공장 PPQ 물량의 매출 인식이 
시작되며 yoy, qoq 외형 성장은 이어질 것으로 예상함.  
파업 당시 생산이 지연된 물량은 3Q 중 추가 생산되어 4Q26부터 매출에 반
영될 전망임. 5공장은 다수 프로젝트의 PPQ가 진행 중이며, Ramp-up에 
따라 매출 기여도 점진적으로 확대될 것으로 예상함. 이에 4Q26에는 파
업 이연 물량 반영과 5공장 매출 확대에 따라 실적 성장폭이 확대될 전
망임. 2026년 연간 매출액과 영업이익은 각각 5조 5,310억원(+21.4% 
yoy), 2조 4,780억원(+19.8% yoy, OPM 44.8%)으로 전망함.

신규 수주 회복 기대, 중장기 성장 기반은 확대 중 
관세 관련 불확실성으로 둔화됐던 고객사와의 수주 논의가 재개되고 있음. 
10월 2일 유럽 소재 제약사와 935억원 규모의 CMO 계약을 추가 체결
했으며, 4Q26 신규 수주 회복 여부에 주목함.  
PolyPeptide 인수는 연내 거래 종결을 목표로 진행 중이며, 인수 완료 
시 펩타이드 분야로 CDMO 포트폴리오가 확대될 전망임. 6 공장도 연내 
착공을 목표로 준비 중임. 
한편, 노사 리스크는 단기 불확실성 요인임. 노조는 10/26부터 3주간 2
차 총파업을 예고함. 1차 대비 예정된 파업 기간이 길어진 만큼 실제 파
업 진행 시 생산 차질 규모가 주요 변수임. 다만 파업 개시 전까지 협상 
가능성이 열려 있어, 향후 노사 협상 진행 경과를 확인할 필요가 있음.

## Document 206: [207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 환율 하락에도 견조한 흐름삼성바이오로직스(207940) 환율 하락에도 견조한 흐름환율 하락에도 견조한 흐름 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 환율 하락에도 견조한 흐름삼성바이오로직스(207940) 환율 하락에도 견조한 흐름환율 하락에도 견조한 흐름 - 기업.pdf

 절대 (달러환산) (7.9) 11.8  (4.8)

3Q26Pre: 환율 하락에도 성장 흐름 유지 
매출액 1조 3,477억원(+7.2%YoY, 이하 YoY 생략), 영업이익 5,957억원(-6.0%, OPM 
44.2%)로 컨센서스에 부합할 것으로 전망. 3분기 원달러 평균 환율은 1,423원으로 전분기 
평균 환율 대비 약 5.2% 하락에도 불구하고 성장 흐름 지속. 
2분기 노조 파업으로 인한 영향은 3분기 생산을 진행했으며 4분기 매출에 반영될 예정으로 
연간 실적에 미치는 영향은 제한적일 것으로 예상. GSK로부터 인수한 록빌 공장도 안정적
으로 생산이 이루어지고 있으며 CAPA 여유분은 추가 수주를 진행 중으로 28~29년 이후 
완전 가동 전망. 
4Q, 6공장 건설과 폴리펩타이드 인수 
유상 증자 관련 투자 설명서 상 투자 계획에 따르면 6공장 투자는 26년부터 29년까지 진행 
될 예정으로 29년 가동 목표. 6공장은 5공장과 동일한 18만 리터 규모로 건설되며, 연내 투
자 집행을 시작함에 따라 건설 발표도 임박한 것으로 추정. 동사는 일정 수준 이상 기존 공
장 수주를 확보한 후 후속 공장 건설을 진행해오고 있어 non-binding 수주 계약의 본계약 
전환도 순차적으로 이루어질 것으로 전망. 
폴리펩타이드 인수도 현재 공개 매수를 진행 중에 있으며 연내 마무리될 것으로 예상. 
1H26 폴리펩타이드 매출은 2.37억 유로(+41.6%)로 GLP-1 agonist 등 펩타이드 의약품 
수요 증가에 따라 높은 성장 지속. 
투자 의견 Buy, 목표 주가 200만원으로 하향 
매수 의견을 유지하며 목표 주가는 기존 210만원에서 200만원으로 하향. 목표 주가 하향은 
유상 증자에 따른 EPS 희석에 기인. 글로벌 항체 CDMO 수요가 여전히 견조하며, 폴리펩
타이드 인수로 펩타이드로의 확장에 따른 추가적인 성장 기대. 주주 대상 유상 증자 영향과 
파업 우려 등이 있으나 노사 합의 시 주가 반등 전망.

## Document 230: AI脅威論にどう向き合うべきか

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI脅威論にどう向き合うべきか.pdf
Report source: DIR
Date: 2026-10-09
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

日本

株式会社大和総研 丸の内オフィス 〒100-6756 東京都千代田区丸の内一丁目 9 番 1 号 グラントウキョウ ノースタワー 
このレポートは投資勧誘を意図して提供するものではありません。このレポートの掲載情報は信頼できると考えられる情報源から作成しておりますが、その正確性、完全
性を保証するものではありません。また、記載された意見や予測等は作成時点のものであり今後予告なく変更されることがあります。㈱大和総研と大和証券㈱は、㈱大和
証券グループ本社を親会社とする大和証券グループの会社です。内容に関する一切の権利は㈱大和総研にあります。無断での複製・転載・転送等はご遠慮ください。

2026 年 10 月 9 日 全8 頁

AI 脅威論にどう向き合うべきか 
安全確保と開発競争の両立に向けた企業間協調の可能性と限界

AI アナリティックリサーチ室  主任研究員 田邉 美穂 
  主任研究員 新田 尭之

[要約] 
 最先端AI の急速な能力向上に伴い、AI がもたらす脅威への警戒が再び強まっている。
2026 年 9 月、Anthropic のダリオ・アモデイ CEO は、安全性の検証や対策が追いつく
範囲に開発速度を抑える必要性を訴え、企業間協調を提案した。企業や政府の間では、
開発ペースの調整や国際的な統制をめぐって立場が分かれる一方、能力評価やインシ
デント報告などでは協調が模索されており、9 月29 日には米国のAI 関連企業6 社が、
ホワイトハウスで安全確保に関する自主的な共同コミットメントに署名した。 
 AI 脅威論には、AI の高度化そのものがもたらす脅威と、AI の開発・保有・普及をめぐ
る脅威という二つの側面がある。前者には、人間による制御の喪失やサイバー攻撃な
どへの悪用があり、後者には、高度なAI 能力の一部の企業・国家への集中や、利用で
きる主体とできない主体間の格差拡大がある。前者に備えて単独で開発を減速すれば
他者に優位を奪われ、危険な能力へのアクセスを制限すれば後者の集中や格差を強め
かねない。安全確保と開発競争の間には、こうした二つのジレンマがある。 
 これらへの対応には企業や国家による協調が必要だが、その難しさは企業間と国家間
で異なる。企業間では他社も同等の安全対策を実施すると確認できれば、能力評価や
情報共有などで協調の余地がある。ただし、協調が機能するには①主要企業の参加、
②検証の信頼性、③副作用の抑制が求められるが、企業間の合意だけではこれらを満
たしにくい。さらに国家間では相手国の減速を確認しにくく、開発を続けることが合
理的になりやすい。このため企業間協調だけでは限界があり、企業間の安全基準や評
価方法を各国の制度に取り込み、各国の言語や利用環境における実効性を独立した立
場から検証できる主体を増やす必要がある。 
 日本に期待されるのは、こうした検証を担い、企業間で形成された安全基準を国際的
な枠組みへ接続する橋渡し役である。日本は広島AI プロセスを通じて国際指針や行動
規範の形成を主導してきたものの、AI セーフティ・インスティテュート（AISI）の本
格的な評価・検証体制の整備はこれからである。AI の能力向上と企業間協調をめぐる
議論が急速に進む中、最先端AI の能力・リスクに関する評価結果や安全対策の実効性
を自ら技術的に検証できる体制を、早急に整えることが求められる。

### Page 2

1

2 / 8

1. AI 開発の減速をめぐって割れる企業と政府 
アモデイ氏の問題提起 
2026 年9 月、Anthropic のダリオ・アモデイCEO は、最先端AI の能力向上が速すぎれば、安
全性の検証や対策が追いつかなくなるとして、主要企業が歩調を合わせて開発ペースを落とす
必要性を訴えた1。背景には、AI が次世代 AI の開発を加速させる再帰的自己改善の進展と、評
価中のAI エージェントが外部システムに侵入する事案が複数の企業で生じたことなどがある。
アモデイ氏は、人間による制御の喪失に加え、サイバー攻撃やバイオテロへの悪用、深刻な経
済的混乱などを主な脅威として挙げている。 
もっとも、アモデイ氏の主張は、AI の開発自体の停止を求めるものではない。むしろ、安全
性の検証に必要な時間を確保しながら開発を進めるため、①開発企業が外部評価者を受け入れ
る、②民主主義国のフロンティアAI 企業が政府の仲介や米国の独占禁止法（反トラスト法）の
限定的な適用除外の下で協調する、③国家間の協調へ広げる、という協調のあり方を提案して
いる。ただし、開発ペースの調整は米国が中国に対する技術的優位を維持できる範囲に限られ
るとし、対中輸出管理やモデル蒸留（他の AI モデルの回答を学習し、別の AI モデルの性能を
高める手法）への対策強化などを前提としている。

AI 企業各社と米国政府の反応 
この問題提起に対し、OpenAI やGoogle DeepMind などは企業間協調に理解を示した。また、
OpenAI のサム・アルトマン CEO は、開発速度を調整する必要性に同意し、独立した評価者に従
業員並みのアクセスを認めるというアモデイ氏の提案について、自社も同様に取り組む方針を
示した2。一方、Meta のマーク・ザッカーバーグCEO は、競争と法的責任が各社による安全確保
を促すとの考えを示し、NVIDIA のジェンスン・フアン CEO も AI のリスクを過度に強調する見
方に否定的な立場を取った3。ただし、Meta も第三者による独立評価には前向きであるほか 、
同社と NVIDIA はいずれも 9 月 29 日の共同コミットメント（後述）には署名している。各社の
立場は、安全確保か開発継続かで単純に二分できるものではない。 
こうした議論は企業間にとどまらず、国際安全保障上の問題へと広がった。9 月 23 日の国連
安全保障理事会では、アルトマン氏、アモデイ氏ら が AI と国際安全保障について意見を述べ
た。アルトマン氏は、人間が AI を制御できなくなることと、強力なAI が少数の企業・国家に
集中することを主要なリスクに挙げ、AI の能力とリスクの評価、人間による監督の維持、イン
シデント報告などに関する国際的な共通基準を求めた4。

1 Dario Amodei“We Must Pace the Frontier”, September 2026. 
2 SiliconANGLE“Sam Altman and Elon Musk back Dario Amodei's call to slow down the frontier of AI 
development”, September 13, 2026. 
3 日本経済新聞 電子版「アンソロピックCEO『競合批判やめる』 AI 暴走脅威に業界結束訴え」（2026 年9
月 16 日） 
4 OpenAI“Sam Altman’s remarks at the United Nations Security Council”, September 23, 2026.

### Page 3

1

3 / 8

これに対し、同じ会合で米国政府は、AI をめぐる国際対話には関与するものの、それが国際
的な統制の枠組みへ発展することには否定的な立場を示した。国際的な枠組みによって米国の
AI 開発が制約されることには否定的である5。 
もっとも、米国政府は、AI のリスクに対する限定的な協力や管理まで否定しているわけでは
ない。翌 24 日の米中首脳会談では、AI のリスクと便益について意見を交換する枠組みと、AI
インシデントに対応する二国間連絡チャネルを設けることで合意した6。開発ペースの調整や共
通規制には踏み込まなかったものの、重大な事態に備えた連絡や情報共有など、両国の利害が
一致しやすい分野では限定的な協力が具体化している。米国内でも、最先端AI の能力評価や外
国への技術流出対策など、安全保障上の管理が進められており、議会には独立評価やインシデ
ント報告を求める法案が提出されている7。さらに9 月 29 日には、Anthropic、OpenAI、Google、
Meta、xAI、NVIDIA の 6 社の代表者 がホワイトハウスで “Joint Commitment on Frontier 
Responsibilities”に署名し、内部統制の強化、外部監査人との連携、基準づくりのための定
期協議を約束した。ただし、これは法的拘束力のない自主的な取り組みであり、トランプ政権
は政府による規制よりも自主規制を重視する姿勢を改めて示した8。 
このように、AI の安全性をめぐっては、企業間・国家間で共通の対応を模索する動きが見ら
れる一方、開発ペースや規制のあり方をめぐる立場には隔たりがある（図表1）。

図表 1 AI 開発と安全確保をめぐる各主体の主な見解

（注）各主体の立場は一様ではなく、安全確保、規制、開発速度、国家間競争の各論点で見解が異なる。 
（出所）各社・各国政府・国連の公表資料、各種報道より大和総研作成

5 United Nations“Ongoing Efforts to Create Powerful AI a ‘Race Where Everyone Loses’, UN Expert 
Tells Security Council, as Members, Tech Leaders Debate Governance Issues”, September 23, 2026. 
6 The White House“Fact Sheet: President Donald J. Trump Advances a Fair and Reciprocal 
Relationship with China While Hosting Historic State Visit”, September 25, 2026. 
7 U.S. Congress“H.R. 9925, Frontier Risk Oversight, National Transparency, Independent Evaluation, 
and Reporting Act (FRONTIER Act)”, July 23, 2026. 
8 Al Jazeera“Trump, tech bosses sign voluntary pact pledging 'robust' AI safeguards”,  September 
29, 2026. 
主な考え方主な主体立場
安全確保の時間を確保するため、
最先端AIの開発速度を調整し、
共通の評価基準などを検討
Anthropic、OpenAI、Googleなど企業間協調を重視
安全確保は各社の責任であり、
過度な規制や開発規制は避けるべきMeta、NVIDIAなど個社対応・市場競争を重視
他国に主導権を奪われるリスクをもつ
開発減速には慎重米国政府（特にトランプ政権）など国家間競争を重視

### Page 4

1

4 / 8

2. AI 脅威論の二つの側面と、安全確保と開発競争のジレンマ 
AI 脅威論①：AI の高度化そのものがもたらす脅威 
AI 脅威論の一つ目の捉え方は、AI の能力が高度化すること自体が、人間や社会に大きな被害
をもたらす可能性に注目するものである。代表的な論点として、AI が人間の意図とは異なる目
標を追求する問題や、人間がAI の判断や行動を十分に監視・制御できなくなる（制御喪失）リ
スクが挙げられる。AI が次世代 AI の開発を支援し、自身の能力向上を加速させる再帰的自己
改善が進めば、人間による評価や安全対策が追いつかなくなるとの懸念もある。もっとも、こ
うした懸念が実際に顕在化する時期や確率を見積もることは難しく、不確実性が大きい。 
一方、近年は、サイバー攻撃の支援や脆弱性の探索、生物学的な情報の生成など、AI が現時
点で持つ危険な能力を、具体的な評価や事案から把握する動きが進んでいる。2026 年夏には、
評価中の AI エージェントが外部システムに侵入する事案が複数の企業で相次いで明らかにな
り、評価環境の外部に影響を及ぼし得る能力が示された9。ただし、これを制御喪失の兆候とみ
るか、安全装置を外した評価環境における封じ込めの失敗とみるかで見方は分かれる。危険な
能力の確認が実社会での大規模な被害に直結するとは限らず、将来的な制御喪失のシナリオと、
現時点で観測される危険な能力を区別して評価する必要がある。

AI 脅威論②：AI の開発・保有・普及がもたらす脅威 
AI 脅威論の二つ目の捉え方は、AI そのものの危険性だけでなく、高度な AI を誰が開発・保
有し、誰が利用できるかが、企業や国家の力関係を変化させることに注目するものである。最
先端AI は、経済成長や情報収集、サイバー・軍事能力などに影響し得るため、その開発・保有
能力は国家安全保障上の優位にもつながり得る。このため、米中間では、AI モデルだけでなく、
半導体、計算資源、クラウド、人材などを含む開発基盤全体が競争の対象となっている。モデ
ル蒸留などをめぐる問題も、知的財産の問題にとどまらず、高度なAI 能力が競合企業や他国へ
移転する経済安全保障上の問題として捉えられている。 
危険な能力へのアクセス制限は、悪用や無制限な拡散を抑えるために必要となり得る一方、
最先端 AI を利用できる国・企業と利用できない主体との格差を広げる可能性がある。 実際、
2026 年 6 月には、米国政府がAnthropic に対して最先端モデルへの外国籍者のアクセスを全面
禁止する輸出規制措置を発出した結果、同社が対象モデルの提供を全世界で一時停止し、日本
からも一時的に利用できなくなった10。また、安全規制への対応に高度な評価能力や多額の費
用が必要になれば、既存大手への集中を強め、新規参入を妨げるおそれもある。このように、
高度なAI 能力が一部の国家・企業に集中することと、危険な能力が無制限に拡散することの双

9 Anthropic“Investigating three real-world incidents in our cybersecurity evaluations”, July 30, 
2026; OpenAI“The Hugging Face incident and the road ahead”, August 26, 2026; among others. 
10 新田尭之・田邉美穂「米国政府はなぜ最先端 AI を停止させたのか：最先端AI モデルへの輸出規制措置が示
す AI 統治の転換点」（大和総研レポート、2026 年6 月17 日）

5 / 8

方がリスクとなり、その能力を誰が保有し、誰が利用や移転を管理するのかが重要な論点とな
っている。

安全確保と開発競争のジレンマと協調を阻む構造 
これら二つの脅威は相互に影響している。AI の能力が高まるほど、安全対策の必要性と、そ
の能力を保有する経済的・安全保障上の価値がともに高まり、企業間・国家間の開発競争が激
しくなる。競争を優先すれば安全対策が後回しになりやすいが、単独で開発ペースを落とせば、
減速しない企業や国家が技術的な優位を得る可能性がある。このため、危険性を認識しても開
発競争から離脱しにくいというジレンマが生じる。 また、危険な能力へのアクセス制限や安全
規制は、悪用や拡散を抑える一方、能力の集中や利用格差 の拡大といった別のジレンマを生み
かねない（図表2）。 
こうした二つのジレンマへの対応には、企業や国家による協調が必要となる。ただし、AI で
は開発の主体と最先端の能力が国家ではなく民間企業に集中しているため、 協調は企業間と国
家間の二つの層で求められ、その難しさもそれぞれ異なる。 
企業間では、各社が安全対策の必要性を共有し、他社も同等の対策を実施すると確認できれ
ば、歩調を合わせる余地がある。相互の協力が期待できる状況では、協調が各社にとって最も
望ましく、抜け駆けする誘因は小さい。こうした安全対策の共通化は、協調の成否が相互の信
頼にかかるという点で、いわゆる「鹿狩りゲーム（全員が協力すれば最大の成果が得られるが、
他者の協力を信じられなければ各自が確実だが小さな成果を選んでしまう状況を表すゲーム理
論のモデル）」に近い構図である。実際、OpenAI、Anthropic、Google は、AI の安全性をめぐる
企業間協調について協議を進めている11。ただし、中国企業やオープンモデルとの競争も続い
ており、協調に加わらない主体が存在すれば、参加企業も 競争に備えざるを得ず、企業間協調
は不安定になる。 
国家間では、相手国が減速すれば抜け駆けして優位に立てる一方、相手国が開発を続ければ
減速した自国だけが後れを取る。このため、相手国の行動にかかわらず、自国は国内企業の開
発を後押しすることが合理的になりやすい。その結果、双方が減速する方が互いに望ましい場
合でも、双方が開発を続ける状態に陥りやすい。これは、いわゆる「囚人のジレンマ（各自が
自分にとって合理的な選択をした結果、全員にとって望ましくない結果に陥る状況を表すゲー
ム理論のモデル） 」に近い。合意によって減速を約束しても、各国には約束を破る誘因が残る
うえに、相手国が守っているかを確認することも難しい。国家間競争は自国企業に開発継続を
促すため、企業間の協調だけでは安全確保と開発競争のジレンマを解消できるとは限らない。

11 CNBC“OpenAI, Google, Anthropic discussing collaboration on AI safety issues”, September 15, 
2026.

図表 2 協調を阻む安全確保と開発競争のジレンマ

3. 企業間協調の可能性と限界、日本の役割 
安全性をめぐる企業間協調は機能するか 
OpenAI、Anthropic、Google DeepMind による協調は、安全対策を共通の競争条件とし、個社
だけが不利益を負う問題を緩和する可能性がある。ただし、協調の対象によって実現可能性は
異なる。能力評価の手法、第三者検証、インシデント報告などの情報共有や共通基準づくりは
比較的進めやすい。一方、開発ペースそのものの合意は競争を直接制約するため、抜け駆けの
誘因が残るほか、独占禁止法（反トラスト法）に抵触するおそれもある。実際、米国では、開
発減速をめぐる各社の呼びかけが違法な競争制限に当たるとして、消費者による集団訴訟が提
起されている12。 
前章で示した協調を阻む構造を踏まえると、企業間協調が機能するには、①主要企業の参加、
②検証の信頼性、③副作用の抑制という三つの条件が求められる。 
一つ目の主要企業の参加では、主要な開発主体が協調に参加し、不参加企業との競争に対処
できることが求められる。不参加主体が開発を続ければ、参加企業にも安全策を緩める誘因が
生じるため、協調の参加範囲を広げるとともに、不参加主体との競争によって合意が崩れない
仕組みが必要となる。 
二つ目の検証の信頼性では、合意した安全策の実施状況を相互に確認できることが求められ
る。他社が合意を守っていると確認できなければ、自社だけが不利益を負うことへの警戒から、
協調は維持されにくい。ただし、最先端モデルを評価できる主体は限られるほか、評価期間の

12 CBS News“Lawsuit says Anthropic, OpenAI, SpaceXAI and Google made illegal deal on AI 
slowdown”, September 19, 2026.

短縮や、評価中のモデルが評価の意図を認識して行動を変える可能性など、検証の信頼性を損
なう要因もある。 
三つ目の副作用の抑制では、協調が市場集中や競争制限につながることを防ぐ必要がある。
前章でみたように、高度で費用のかかる安全基準は既存大手への集中を強めかねない。また、
政府や評価機関が主要企業の知見や資源に依存すれば、企業の利益が過度に制度に反映される
おそれもある。このため、基準形成の透明性や外部主体の関与を確保し、安全性を理由とする
協調が既存企業の地位を守り、市場競争を制限する手段とならないようにする必要がある。 
企業間の自主的な合意だけでは、不参加企業に網をかけられず、検証の独立性や基準づくり
の透明性も確保しにくい。このため、三つの条件を満たしていくには、企業間で形成された安
全基準や評価方法を各国の制度に取り込み、国際的な枠組みを通じて共有する必要がある 。国
家間で開発減速に合意するなど、開発ペースそのものを調整することは難しくとも、能力評価
やインシデント報告の基準を共有し、各国が共通の基準に基づいて企業の対応を検証する仕組
みは構築し得る。 
こうした仕組みの実効性を確保するには、企業間で形成された基準を国際的に共有するだけ
でなく、各国の言語や利用環境において安全策が機能するかを確認し、その実施状況を独立し
た立場から検証できる主体を増やす必要がある。

日本は国際的な枠組みづくりで存在感を示せるか 
最先端 AI の開発を米国企業などが先導する中でも、日本が AI の安全性をめぐる国際的な枠
組みの形成に関与する余地は残されている。日本は、2023 年 5 月の G7 広島サミットを契機に
立ち上げられた広島 AI プロセスを通じて、高度な AI を開発する組織に求める国際指針や行動
規範の形成を主導してきた13。広島 AI プロセスでは、国際行動規範への対応状況を各組織が自
主的に報告・公表する「報告枠組み」も運用されており、各社のリスク管理や安全対策を国際
的に可視化・比較するための基盤が形成されている 14。ただし、企業間で形成された安全基準
を各国の制度へ接続する段階では、こうした原則や枠組みを提唱した実績だけで、日本が引き
続き国際的な枠組み形成に実質的に関与できるとは限らない。 
前節で整理したとおり、企業間で形成された安全基準や評価方法を国際的な枠組みへ発展さ
せるには、各国の言語や利用環境における実効性を 独立した立場から検証できる主体を増やす
必要がある。評価・検証に必要なのは、評価対象モデルへのアクセス、評価手法や評価環境、
専門人材であり、自国で最先端AI を開発する能力を有することは、必ずしも前提とならない。
日本が国際的な枠組みの形成に実質的に関与するには、まずは最先端AI の能力・リスクに関す
る評価結果や、安全対策の実効性を自ら技術的に検証できる体制を整えることが重要となる。 
日本には、その足掛かりとなる実績や知見がある。日本政府が 2024 年に設立した、AI の安

13 広島 AI プロセス「広島AI プロセスについて」（2026 年10 月5 日最終閲覧） 
14 広島 AI プロセス「報告枠組み」（2026 年10 月5 日最終閲覧）

1
8 / 8
全性に関する評価や基準策定を担う機関、AI セーフティ・インスティテュート（AISI）15は、
10 か国が参加するAI モデルの国際共同テスト演習に加わり、複数の言語におけるLLM（大規模
言語モデル）の評価手法や AI エージェントの評価手法の改善に取り組んできた16。日本が知見
を蓄積しつつある領域である。
このように、日本には評価・検証の足掛かりとなる実績があるものの、それを本格的に担う
ための体制整備はこれからである。AISI はこれまで、評価基準やガイドといった「物差しづく
り」を重視してきた。2025 年の年次レポートでは、今後の方向性として「自ら評価する能力を
もつ」ことと「国際協調を主導する」ことを掲げている17。日本政府も 2026 年 7 月に改定・閣
議決定した「人工知能基本計画」において、AISI の抜本的強化を引き続き掲げている。同年 6
月には、将来的に AISI が最先端 AI などを評価できる体制の構築に向け、必要な手法や評価環
境を検討する調査が開始された18。

日本に期待されるのは、企業間で形成された安全基準をそのまま受け入れるのではなく、各
国の言語や利用環境における実効性を検証し、国際的な枠組みへ接続する際の橋渡し役である。
そのためには、AISI の専門人材や評価環境の充実に加え、公開前を含む評価対象モデルへのア
クセス、機微情報を扱うための情報管理体制、他国のAI 安全機関との共同評価体制を整える必
要がある。AI の能力向上と企業間協調をめぐる議論が急速に進む中、最先端 AI を自ら評価す
る日本の体制づくりはなお調査・検討段階にある。このままでは、最先端 AI の開発だけでな
く、その安全性をめぐる国際的な枠組みの形成でも後れを取りかねない。広島AI プロセスで培
った実績を活かし、国際的な枠組みづくりに継続的に関与していくためにも、最先端 AI の能
力・リスクに関する評価結果や安全対策の実効性を、自ら技術的に検証できる体制を早急に整
えることが求められる。

## Document 242: Anthropic被曝秘密游说国会：AI造成超10亿美元损失，政府才能叫停？

Source PDF: all report pdf/Anthropic被曝秘密游说国会：AI造成超10亿美元损失，政府才能叫停？.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

据 Politico 报道， Anthropic 正秘密向国会游说⼀项前沿 AI 安全监管法案草案。草案规定，
仅当模型存在造成逾 10 亿美元损失或超 50 ⼈伤亡的预测性即时⻛险时，政府⽅可⼲预。草
案要求开发者开展独⽴评估与透明化测试，并由指定法院最终裁决是否暂停模型。
摘要：
Anthropic 正悄然推动⼀项由其主导起草的 AI 安全法案，试图为联邦政府⼲预前沿 AI 模型设定极
⾼⻔槛，同时将监管权⼒严格限定于商务部和联邦法院。
据路透社 17 ⽇援引 Politico 报道， Anthropic 代表已私下向国会议员游说这份草案法规。根据法案
内容，政府只有在 AI 模型⾯临可预⻅的、迫在眉睫的⻛险 —— 即可能造成超过 10 亿美元损失，
或导致 50 ⼈以上死亡或重伤时，⽅可介⼊阻⽌模型发布或使⽤。这⼀⾼⻔槛意味着，绝⼤多数
情形下 AI 模型的开发与部署将不受政府⼲预。
Anthropic 发⾔⼈向路透社表⽰，该法案所体现的提案内容，源⾃ Anthropic 今年早些时候发布

的 " 先进 AI 框架 " （ Advanced AI Framework ）。发⾔⼈同时强调，法案将赋予政府 " 阻⽌或遏制最
强⼤模型危险部署的能⼒ " ，并要求开发者对前沿 AI 模型进⾏测试、公开评估结果，并接受独⽴
第三⽅评估。商务部未就路透社的置评请求作出回应。
触发监管的条件极为严苛
根据 Politico 的报道，法案对政府介⼊条件作出严格限定。触发监管的⻛险必须源于以下三种特
定情形之⼀： AI 模型被⽤于⽣物武器的研制或扩散、模型逃脱开发者的控制，或模型引发⽹络
攻击。
这意味着，仅仅是模型可能带来的⼴泛社会影响或经济竞争扭曲，并不构成政府叫停的法定依
据。批评者可能认为，上述条件设置过于狭窄，实际上为 Anthropic 等前沿 AI 实验室的持续运营
提供了相当程度的法律屏障。
独⽴评估机构居核⼼地位，但由实验室付费
法案设计了⼀套以独⽴评估机构为核⼼的监督机制。这些评估机构须在商务部登记注册，并负
责定期对实验室及其前沿模型进⾏评估。
值得关注的是，法案允许 AI 实验室向这些评估机构⽀付费⽤，但明确规定报酬不得与评估结论挂

钩。⼀旦独⽴评估机构判定某⼀前沿模型存在 " 迫在眉睫的列举⻛险 " ，须在完成评估后 72 ⼩时内
向商务部报告。
这⼀付费安排或引发外界对评估独⽴性的质疑，尽管法案试图以 " 报酬与结论脱钩 " 的条款加以防
范。
监管权⼒⾼度集中，其他机构明确排除在外
在权⼒边界上，法案作出了明确且严格的划定。商务部可在认定⻛险后向法院申请暂停模型运
营，由哥伦⽐亚特区联邦地区法院的联邦法官作出最终裁决。
然⽽，法案同时明⽂禁⽌美国其他任何政府部⻔、机构或官员暂停 AI 模型的开发、部署或内部使
⽤。这⼀条款将监管权⼒⾼度集中于商务部和特定联邦法院，有效阻⽌了其他联邦机构 —— 包
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⽣活家 搜索

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

写评论
图⽚
括国防部、国家安全机构等 —— 对 AI 模型采取独⽴⾏动。
对投资者⽽⾔，若该法案最终⽴法，意味着前沿 AI 模型在美国市场将享有较⾼程度的运营确定
性，监管⼲预⻛险受到法律层⾯的严格约束。

Sector Report | Technology｜Oct 15, 2026 
Oct Edition: Choppy Markets, Intact AI

Overseas  
Technology

Jeff Pu, CFA 
SFC CE No. BNO719 
jeffpu@gfgroup.com.hk 
Henry Huang 
SFC CE No. BYA749 
henryhuang@gfgroup.com.hk

Market to remain choppy: Tech equities were volatile in the past month, with rotation 
toward hyperscaler and internet names. That said , s emi and memory (Korea) 
weakness reflected lower risk appetite due to rising bond yields, AI monetization/ARR 
visibility and L-T AI debates (i.e., Broadcom CEO warned that capital may now be a 
bigger constraint), even as company-specific catalysts continued to be strong. On the 
other hand, the positive was the acceleration of AI model scaling (i.e., Meta’s Muse 
Wonder, Gemini 4, etc). That said, w e view the pullback primarily as a reset in 
expectations rather than the evidence of AI deterioration. Despite light positioning, we 
expect the markets will remain choppy in the near term but would  accumulate low 
valuation & sustainable names  (i.e., HBM, non-TSMC foundries, CPUs, CPOs, etc) . 
In our preferred list, we added CLS, while removing MTK and Hon Hai.  
U.S. roadshow takeaways (week of Oct 5): Investor tone skewed neutral to slightly 
cautious, driven by conservative top ‑down macro views, U.S. election, and ongoing 
debates around the L-T AI demand. Positioning in AI infrastructure has come off peak 
levels. Even  so, many investors would accumulate on pullbacks, with interest 
concentrated in “Made in U.S.” names (INTC, LITE, COHR), U.S. memory suppliers, 
TSMC, and selective bottleneck themes — including ASE, ABF substrates, 
MRVL/SMTC, and Japanese MLCCs. 
OCP & earnings season in focus: The week is anchored by AI/semis — SEMICON 
West, OCP, and earnings from ASML, TSMC, etc. OCP reinforced the industry’s pivot 
toward rack‑scale infrastructure, with inference and agentic workloads driving demand 
across compute, memory, and networking. Scale‑up optics, NPO/CPO, and 800VDC 
power were key themes, while AMD’s CPU outlook and Helios/Helion underscored a 
secular CPU uptrend. Nvidia ’s use of PTFE for NVLink boards further supports our 
positive stance on PTFE (see PCB Spec in 2 H27, Oct 5).  On earnings, ASML 
delivered largely in -line results, GM and OP above consensus with revenue slightly 
miss, and raised full year guidance again and is exploring ways to increase capacity 
by >30% 2028 to fulfill strong customer demand, also extending the order visibility 
beyond 2028. For TSMC, we anticipate an inline quarter. 
September monthly sales/data: TSMC sales -0.6% MoM, slightly ahead. Rubin to 
accelerate and T3 ramp. PC forecast trimmed. Substrate/CCL upcycle with 
pricing/margin expansion. Aspeed revenue +101% YoY. Details on page 5. 
GFHK Tech Most Read:  1) Jeff Pu on ASIC Updates on TPU v9 ; 2) Michelle Jing 
on PCB Spec in 2H27; 3) Alicia Xia on CPO Acceleration; 4) Jeff Pu on NAND Re-
acceleration; 5) Jeff Pu on TSMC 3Q26 Preview.

### Page 3

Sector Report | Technology

GFHK Tech Important Stock Calls  
In the past one month, our key reports and stock calls are: 
Jeff Pu on ASIC Updates on TPU v9, reported date on Oct 13: We expect near-term MTK headwinds 
from TPU v9 delays but remain constructive on MTK/MRVL longer term. Humufish’s redesign could 
push tape-out to Jan -27, MP tape -out to 2Q27 and shipments to 1Q28, while EMIB -T yields could 
recover from 45–50%. Rising TPU allocations to Anthropic could constrain Gemini compute, though 
higher internal allocation in 2H26 may support future models. SemiAnalysis expects Anthropic/OpenAI 
to each add 5 –10GW in 2027 vs. a few GW for Google. For TPU v10 (4Q28 /1Q29), MTK/GUC’s 
Icefish remains the only confirmed mainstream SKU, while Broadcom-led Merope faces AMD/Marvell 
competition, with both potentially testing EMIB-T. 
Michelle Jing on PCB Spec in 2H27, reported date on Oct 5: Some market watchers expect Nvidia 
to shift from PTFE to hydrocarbon resin (HC) for the VR300 NVL576 switch tray. HC—one of the glass 
free alternatives within PTFE hybrid architectures—has only recently passed SI testing and has yet to 
enter PCB MP, leaving it roughly three months behind PTFE in qualification. On the contrary, we 
believe that PTFE offers superior performance for high frequency, high speed designs. With VR300 
NVL576 shipments expected in 2H27 and the final configuration likely set by end 26 —and with a 
leading Chinese PCB ma ker already achieving meaningful PTFE hybrid yields —we maintain that 
PTFE hybrid remains the most probable solution for next year’s switch board. 
Alicia Xia on CPO Acceleration , reported date on  Sept 29 : Nvidia’s CPO scale -out switch is 
progressing faster than expected, with FAU yields improving. We forecast CPO switch shipments at 
15k/16k/30k in 4Q26/1Q27/2Q27 and 100k in 2027. For Nvidia/AWS scale -up, CPO and NPO 
continue in parallel, with full NPO adoption expected at NVL576 launch. Feynman is also devel oping 
both variants, with CPO potentially using QM6 + six 6.4T OEs. We shift AWS Trainium 4 launch to 
1Q28 and forecast global scale-up OE demand at 6.5m/42m in 2027E/28E. 
Jeff Pu on NAND Re -acceleration, reported date on Sept 20 : According to TrendForce, NAND 
demand is stronger than expected as U.S. CSPs scale AI infrastructure, with eSSD demand nearly 
2x prior forecasts. We raise NAND contract price growth to +21%/+17%/+15% QoQ in 
3Q26/4Q26/1Q27, while hyperscaler inventories decline. Supply remains disci plined at 15%/21%/20% 
YoY bit growth in 2025 –27. Demand is supported by agentic AI, vector databases and KV -cache 
offloading, where eSSD offers lower cost/bit than DRAM and better power efficiency than HDD. 
Jeff Pu on TSMC 3Q26 Preview , reported date on  Oct 7 : TSMC shares hit record highs on 
expectations ahead of the print and potential Terafab collaboration. We expect largely inline 3Q26 
results, capex and full-year guidance, with softer 4Q26 margin (67.2%). Management should reaffirm 
2026 capex at the ceiling and mid-40s YoY growth, while we forecast 37% growth in 2027 on sustained 
AI/CPU/networking demand and price hikes. Key focuses include margins, long -term capex/U.S. 
expansion, large-reticle packaging and A14. We raise 2026E/27E EPS by 2%/8% on hig her N2/N3 
capacity and operating leverage, and roll to 20x 2027E P/E, lifting TP to NT$2,960.

### Page 5

Sector Report | Technology

September Monthly Data  
TSMC September sales of NT$511.9bn, -0.6% MoM and +54.6% YoY, with total 3Q revenue reaching 
NT$1.49T, +17.6 QoQ, slightly ahead of expectations of +14%. The solid performance was well 
supported by ASIC, A20 Pro, and Rubin. For 3Q26, we expect gross margin to be 67.8%, higher than 
guidance of 65-67% and consensus of 66.4% . For 4Q26, we expect another 12% QoQ growth with 
GM at 67.2%, both ahead of street’s estimates of +10% QoQ and 66% margin. Ahead of the earnings 
call, key focus include gross margin trajectory, long -term capex discipline, and the execution of its 
U.S. fab expansion. 
Rubin to accelerate:  Hon Hai revenue last month was NT$1.16T, +26 % MoM, +38% YoY, mainly 
driven by iPhone build.  Quanta and Wistron delivered -23% and -17% MoM respectively, due to AI 
racks’ shipping timing differences. Of note, Pegatron surprised the market with +143.2% MoM revenue 
surge fueled by initial GB300 rack shipments to neocloud customers including Lambda and Together 
AI. This reaffirms our thesis of robust NVL72 order momentum across Neoclouds. Looking ahead, 
VR200 is ramping now, with volume deployment accelerating into 2027. We maintain our forecast of 
75k and 92k NVL72 (GB+VR) rack shipments for 2026 and 2027, respectively. Concurrently, Wiwynn 
delivered another solid month with revenue expanding 20.0% MoM, underpinne d by resilient GP 
server demand alongside the ramp of Trainium 3. 
ASIC ramping up: On design service vendors fronts, driven  by T3 ramp, Alchip’s Sep revenue 
reached a record NT$9.9 8bn. Backed by secured incremental 3nm wafer allocation and improving 
yields, management raised its 2027 revenue guidance, now targeting growth above the previous 50% 
YoY baseline. That said, we push out T4 shipment timeline into 1Q28. GUC delivered a strong beat 
with monthly revenue reaching a record NT$7.145bn (+22.4% MoM, +97.8 % YoY), driven by robust 
Axion 2 CPU demand. Beyond its engagement with top 4 CSPs, multi -year order visibility into 2027 
and beyond is further bolstered by high-margin (likely TK1) automotive ASIC designs. 
PC forecast trimmed: NB shipments from top 5 ODMs were 9.7m in Sep, with 3Q26 shipments down 
17% QoQ and 26% YoY. Global PC OEM shipments contracted 20.1% YoY in 3Q26, worse than 
expected, post robust 1H26 pull -ins with high pricing points and weak macro. For 2027, we project 
the industry will decline a further 5% YoY. On the competitive front, tier -1 vendors such as Lenovo, 
Dell, and HP are not immune to this downturn, while entry-level laptops (low cost models from China) 
continue to see stronger relative demand.  
Substrate/CCL Upcycle Intact: Unimicron continued to deliver another all-time high sales with +5% 
MoM and 65% YoY. Supported by ABF upward pricing of ~15% in 2H26 (likely more next year) and 
higher mix of AI products, we expect a meaningful GM uptick for this quarter with momentum to sustain 
into 2027 . In the meantime, ZengDing’s revenue rose 28% MoM and 36% YoY mainly driven by  
iPhone, 1.6T mSAP, and Rubin’s HDI. On the CCL front, bolstered by ongoing price hikes and strong 
demand from GP servers, ITEC revenue grew 15.5% MoM and 111% YoY, and NanYa Plastics 
recorded +12.5% MoM and +55% YoY. Meanwhile, EMC and TUC both posted another single digit 
growth MoM on sales.

Downstream tech – Smartphone & Apple  
We maintain Global smartphone forecast of -14% in 2026  
According to IDC, the preliminary 2Q26 smartphone data points to a still ‑soft market with uneven 
regional dynamics, and their commentary continues to emphasize structural rather than cyclical 
headwinds. Smartphone shipments remain under pressure from weak replacement cycles, cautious 
consumer spending, and limited breakthrough features. IDC typically highlights that “growth is 
concentrated in the premium tier,” driven by ecosystem lock ‑in, while the mass market remains 
challenged by price sensitivity. They also tend to note that China and parts of emerging markets show 
only modest stabilization, not a full recovery, and that vendors are prioritizing margin discipline over 
volume.  
Overall, by factoring in higher iPhone 17/17 Pro volume, we fine tune our global smartphone shipment 
forecast to -14% YoY from -15% YoY. 
For 2027, we expect global smartphone shipments to be flattish YoY. That said, 2026 -2027 will be 
one of the most challenging periods for the industry, driven primarily by sharply higher memory prices, 
which have already pushed OEMs to reduce shipments and raise device prices.

iPhone 18 Pro/18 PM demand lukewarm  
iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max wait-time lukewarm: iPhone 18 Pro and iPhone 18 Pro Max pre-orders began 
on Sept 12 in the U.S. and many other countries. Generally speaking, the wait-time appears lukewarm, 
with: 1) 3–4 weeks for 256GB/512GB Pro Max across all colors, as well as Burgundy 1TB in the U.S.；
2) 2–3 weeks for Glacier 256GB/512GB Pro Max in China; 3) 1–2 weeks for Black/Glacier 1TB Pro 
Max in the U.S. ；4) immediate availability for Silver 1TB and mos t 2TB Pro Max models in the U.S, 
as well as most iPhone 18 Pro configurations except for Burgundy 256GB in the U.S. We attribute the 
lukewarm wait-time to limited spec upgrade, higher pricing (especially for large storage models), etc. 
We also expect the Pro Max to be impacted by iPhone Duo and the criticism of heavy weight.  
iPhone Duo feedback generally positive 
The iPhone Duo, priced at $1,999 in the U.S., opened pre‑orders on Oct 16 and officially launched on 
Oct 23. Early reviews were positive, highlig hting its polished folding experience, high ‑quality inner 
display, and improved multitasking in iOS 27, but also point to trade ‑offs including a heavier and 
thicker form factor, a visible crease, and a dual ‑camera. Due to hinge constraints, we are reducing  
the iPhone Duo EMS build plan from 7m to 6m units in 2026. We also lower the iPhone 18 Pro/Pro 
Max build plan to 72m units given the constraints associated with the adjustable‑aperture camera.

Sector Report | Technology

Downstream tech – PC 
Global PC units down to -12% in 2026, followed by another decline in 2027 
On Oct 8, IDC reported 3Q26 global PC shipments fell 20.1% YoY, missing street expectations of a 
10–15% decline. The sharper contraction was primarily driven by front -loaded demand in 1H26, 
elevated ASPs, and broader macro weakness. This aligns with our prior view that ODM build 
momentum moderated starting in end -2Q26, amid the rising concerns over channel inventory. 
Meanwhile, the top 5 ODMs also saw 3Q26 shipments decline 26% YoY. Looking ahead, we model 
4Q26 shipments to continue 20% YoY drop (flattish QoQ), underperforming normal seasonality. 
On the component front, we expect memory de -spec to normalize. End -users still require sufficient 
DRAM capacity (16GB+) to handle on -device AI workloads, and PC DDR 5 supply constraints have 
eased compared to Server’s. On pricing, new models will still have higher pricing points to pass 
through 2H component cost increases across memory, CPU, etc. That said, we anticipate price hikes 
will remain measured vs 1H26 given sluggish end-market demand. 
Overall, reflecting weaker than expected 3Q26 results post pull -ins and a muted 4Q outlook, we trim 
our 2026 PC shipment forecast to -12% YoY. For 2027, we project shipments to decline further by 5% 
YoY, as higher ASP and persistent macro headwinds continue to weigh on demand.

manufactured in China continue to gain traction across China and Southeast Asia. Looking ahead, we 
expect value-tier models to remain the primary driver in sustaining ongoing PC shipments.

Upstream tech – AI Semi & Supply Chain  
U.S. CSP capex to be +85%/+53%/+20% in 2026/2027/2028 
Given capex expands further into 2027, CSPs free cash flow are undergoing significant pressure and 
turning negative except Microsoft, such that shifting to debt financing for their massive capex. In 2025, 
Top 5 CSPs’ total debt issuance reached $108bn, or 24% of total capex . Per our Addressing 2028 
Capex and AI Platform Value Shift report on Aug 6, we forecast Top 5 CSPs’ debt issuance to reach 
$257bn/$419bn in 2026/2027, or 31%/35% of total capex.  Assuming debt issuance to grow in 
2028/2029 at a mild pace, we believe U.S. CSPs' balance sheet (except Oracle) to remain very healthy, 
due to net leverage (EBITDA basis) of only 0.5 and the net debt-to-equity ratio of only 18% by 2029.  
Despite healthy balance sheet for most CSPs, we expect most to safeguard balance sheets beyond 
2027 amid widening spreads and FCF profiles, with the focus shifting to returns. Together with a swing 
factor of competition from Chinese LLMs, we expect U.S. CSPs to decelerate capex expansion. We 
forecast Top 5 CSPs capex of $1,512bn/1,739bn in 2028/2029, or +20%/15% YoY, down from +53% 
in 2027.

Server CPU TAM to grow at 49% CAGR by 2030 
As the orchestration core of AI infrastructure buildouts, the Server CPU has gained significant traction 
over the past few months. This is driven by the Agentic AI/inference trend, fueled by the breakthrough 
of OpenClaw and Anthropic’s ARR surging to $44bn  in April. For instance, in latest earnings calls, 
AMD projected a >35% CAGR for Server CPU TAM by 2030, and Intel said the shift toward agentic 
AI is driving a structural demand for CPU, where the GPU-to-CPU ratio is tightening from 8:1 in training 
to 4:1 in inference. Based on our estimates, assuming inference accounts for 90% of AI workloads 
and GPU-to-CPU ratio for AI servers to reach 1:1.2 in 2030, we now expect Server CPU TAM to grow 
96%/71% in 2026/2027, reaching $215bn by 2030 from $30bn in 2025, with a 5-yr CAGR of 49%.

CoWoS tracker: fine tuning upwardly for total capacity 
Following our earlier raise in May Edition on May 12 in which we revised up TSMC’s CoWoS capacity 
from 175KPM to 195KPM by end-2027, the Street has moved up to our numbers , and we have fine 
tune our estimate to 200KPM by end-2027 on our Sept Edition on Sept 14.  
For OSAT, we maintain our earlier view for Amkor’s capacity of 15KPM/25KPM/55KPM by end of 
2026/2027/2028, and ASE’s CoWoS capacity of 15KPM/45KPM/55KPM by end 2026/2027/2028.  
For fabless, we keep our CoWoS estimates largely unchanged for AI GPU/ASIC. For our accelerator 
model, we maintain +98% YoY 2027E, and for 2028E, we now expect it to reach the TAM of $1,000bn.  
In our ASIC Update report on Oct 13, we lowered our TPU units in 2028 to 10m, vs. 8.9m in 2027.

Sector Report | Technology

Upstream tech – Optical 
Strong demand for pluggable through 2028. 
We expect that the total demand for 800G/1.6T will reach 90mn/80mn in 2027, increased by 
80%/167%+, mainly driven by: 1) Nvidia, Google and AWS and other ASIC’s Accelerators’ demand 
are ramping up; 2) GPU/ASIC scale -out bandwidth continuously  increase drives increasing 
GPU/ASIC-to-optical module ratio; 3) We expect that TPU for 2027 will be 8.9m and Google fully 
transitions to large -scale clusters in 2026 and the scale -up layer will adopt optical interconnects. In 
addition, as we expect that CPO/NPO will start since 2H27, represents a pure incremental opportunity 
for optical interconnects supply chain, benefiting FAU, laser, shuffle box, connector and OE players, 
including LITE, GLW, TSEM, Sumitomo and Browave. We also like Marvell and SMTC, for the 
TIA/Driver used in NPO OE.  
Scale-out bandwidth increase driving optical-module deployment 
For NVIDIA, each Rubin GPU is equipped with two CX9 NIC chips, doubling the scale-out bandwidth 
compared with Blackwell. We further estimate that Rubin Ultra will adopt four CX9 chips per GPU, 
leading to another 2 times increase in scale-out bandwidth, continuing NVIDIA’s consistent bandwidth-
doubling trajectory. Accordingly, we expect the ratio of 1.6T optical modules has risen from 1:2.5 to 
1:5 in Rubin. From CoWoS perspective, we expect 5m/2m Blackwell/Rubin and 20m 1.6T in 2026. 
For Google, we believe that Goo gle fully transition to large -scale clusters in 2026 and the scale -up 
layer will adopt optical interconnects. We believe that in the scale-up domain, the ratio of TPU to 1.6T 
optical modules is approximately 1:1.5; In the scale-out domain, the ratio is around 1:2.5, suggesting 
the overall TPU-to-optics ratio is approximately 1:4 (1.6T equivalent).

Nvidia/CSPs’ Scale-out/up CPO/NPO  
Within NVIDIA’s next -gen NVL576 and broader CSPs’ AI infrastructure plans, CPO and NPO are 
developed side -by-side to expand GP U/ASIC interconnect bandwidth: CPO is the ideal long -term 
design for lower power and latency yet faces headwinds from complicated advanced packaging, and 
yield risks, while NPO could deliver most short -reach optical performance gains with far better 
manufacturability and deployment flexibility, making NPO a reliable near -term scaling path and CPO 
the ultimate end -state architecture. NPO partially removes module -side DSP and shifts signal 
conditioning and equalization to system designs, greatly lifting the value of TIAs and laser drivers that 
take over part of traditional DSP functions; we estimate each 3.2T optical engine contains tens of 
dollars worth of combined TIA and driver chips, a clear improvement over standard pluggable optics. 
Our shipment forecasts at 6.5m/42.4m NPO optical engines in CY27/28.  
Scale-up domain to increase by NVL576 to compensate lower memory: With lower memory spec, we 
expect Nvidia to increase the scale -up domain by optics to enhance performance. According to 
Nvidia’s GTC 2026, th e optical scale -up fabric allows Nvidia to expand Rubin Ultra to an NVL576 
scale-up. Regarding CPO and NPO which are being parallelly developed, we expect NPO to be a 
high reliability scaling path for NPO. We now expect its scale -up to adopt NPO in Rubin U ltra and 
Feynman platform and estimate OE shipments of Nvidia’s platform to be 6.0m/19.7m in 2027/2028.

### Page 25

Sector Report | Technology

Nvidia’s NPO OE to be 6.0m/19.7m in 2027/2028 
For Nvidia, we now expect its scale up to adopt NPO in Rubin Ultra and Feynman platform and 
estimate OE  shipments of Nvidia’s platform to be 6.0m/19.7m in 2027/2028, based on full NPO 
adoption at NVL576 launch given NPO maturity. Coupled with other CSP’s platform, we estimate total 
OE market to be 6.5m/42.4m in 2027/2028.  
TIA/Driver to benefit from NPO 
NPO partially removes module -side DSP and shifts signal conditioning and equalization to system 
designs, greatly lifting the value of TIAs and laser drivers that take over part of traditional DSP 
functions; we estimate each 3.2T optical engine contains tens o f dollars worth of combined TIA and 
driver chips, a clear improvement over standard pluggable optics.  
A key architectural shift under NPO is the partial removal of DSP functionality at the module level, with 
signal conditioning and equalization increasing ly redistributed into system -level design. This 
significantly elevates the importance of TIA (transimpedance amplifier) and laser driver components, 
which effectively replace a portion of traditional DSP value. We estimate that each 3.2T optical engine 
will embed high tens of dollars of combined TIA and driver content, representing a meaningful step -
up versus traditional pluggable optics architectures.  
From a competitive standpoint, we expect Marvell to remain the primary beneficiary of both NPO OE 
given i ts stronger positioning high speed TIA/Driver. Semtech is well positioned as a secondary 
beneficiary through its exposure to high-speed analog signal chain components, particularly TIAs and 
laser drivers, which scale directly with optical engine proliferat ion under NPO. We forecast 
6.5mn/42.4mn NPO OE shipments in CY27/28. Based on our estimated front-end content per OE and 
a 30% market share assumption for Semtech, we estimate its NPO revenue of $315/936mn. 
Stock Implication 
Generally speaking, scale-up CPO/NPO is an incremental opportunity for optical interconnect supply 
chain, as it aims to replace copper and has no impact on scale -out side. Additionally, scale up and 
scale out CPO solution share the similar suppliers. That said, we expect the key benefic iaries to be 
components such as FAU, CW laser, ELSFP, shuffle, PIC, TIA and Driver. We: 1) prefer Lumentum/ 
COHR due to the demand upside for CW laser, and also like local cw laser players; 2) expect Browave 
(3163 TT) to benefit, given the sizable contribu tion of CPO’s shuffle box considering ~55% market 
share, $5-6k ASP and a volume ramp in 2H26; 3) we also like TSEM, given CSP preferring NPO 
solution for their ASICs scale up interconnection. 4) expect GLW to benefit, given the second supplier 
of shuffle b ox and main supplier of MMC connector/fiber. 5) Marvell/Semtech will also benefit from 
CSP/Nvidia’s NPO solution, since TIA and driver will upgrade in the NPO Optical Engine.

### Page 28

Sector Report | Technology

Google’s 6D Torus & Beneficiaries.  
We expect Google will migrate its TPU interconnect from 3D to 6D torus, re -organizing the 64 -chip 
elemental block from 4× 4× 4 to 2× 2× 2× 2× 2× 2. External optical links per block rise from 96 to 384, with 
optical ports per chip scaling increase from 1:1.5 to 1:6, driving 4x coherent lite transceivers and OCS 
demand. The trajectory aligns with NVIDIA's CPO/NPO move. Both are interconnect scaling that 
expands direct chip-to-chip bandwidth and reduces hop count, with TPU via a 6D torus and NVIDIA 
via CPO/NPO for Scale-up. 
Google’s migration from 3D  to 6D Torus: We expect Google to migrate its TPU interconnect from a 
3D to a 6D torus topology, re-organizing the elemental 64-chip building block from a 4× 4× 4 3D Torus 
cube into a 2× 2× 2× 2× 2× 2 hyper-cube. In the current 3D design, each chip connects to 6 nearest 
neighbors across X/Y/Z axes and the cube exposes 16 optical ICI links on each of its 6 faces, totaling 
96 external optical links per cube (~1.5 optical ports per chip). Under the 6D design, each dimension 
has 64 external optical links (2⁶ chips, on e outward link per chip per dimension), for a total of 6 ×  64 
= 384 external optical links (~6 optical ports per chip). The per-chip optical port count thus scales from 
1.5 to 6. Combined with Scale -out at an estimated 1:2.5, this brings the aggregate TPU -to-port ratio 
to 1:8.5. 
Lower latency and closer to non -blocking Scale-up: For a 4,096 -chip superpod, a 3D torus (4× 4× 4) 
carries a worst-case diameter of 24 hops, whereas a 6D torus (2× 2× 2× 2× 2× 2) halves that to 12 hops 
(2× 6), directly compressing collective-operation and latency. Higher dimensionality also spreads traffic 
across 6 orthogonal paths, improving bisection bandwidth and moving closer to non-blocking chip-to-
chip communication. Critically, shortening each edge from 4 to 2 nodes eliminates deep mu lti-hop 
electrical paths inside the block: the architecture deliberately reduces intra -cube copper 
communication and pushes a far larger share of traffic onto optical fiber. 
Key beneficiaries: The exact TPU generation for the adoption of 6D torus and initi al penetration rate 
remain unclear. That said, whether NVIDIA moves toward CPO/NPO or Google expands from 3D to 
6D, both are Scale -up strategies aimed at enabling greater inter -chip communication. Key 
beneficiaries include the 2.4T Coherent Lite module and the OCS supply chain, such as MEMS-based 
optical switch vendors, PIC/laser/DSP suppliers, and fiber/cabling vendors supporting the larger 
reconfigurable lightwave fabric.

Risks 
1) Alternative technologies; 2) AI demand and capex slowdown; 3) IT demand slowdown; 4) product 
delays.

### Page 4

◆ The economy remains on the recovery track despite weakness in some
areas
Annualized real GDP growth in the second quarter was +1.1% QoQ, with growth
staying in positive territory for three straight quarters. Although personal
consumption exhibited weakness and capital investment declined, overall growth
was lifted by foreign demand. Exports, particularly of services, increased, and the
drop in imports of Middle Eastern crude oil also helped foreign demand turn positive.

◆ Housing starts are weak
Housing starts surged 18.6% YoY in June. They have continued to rise on a YoY
basis, but the jump was mainly due to a rebound from the slump in the same month
of the previous year. The recovery in the number of starts is actually moderate,
reflecting higher interest rates and soaring construction costs.
◆ Earthquake impact will need to be watched
Damage from the earthquake that occurred in Kumamoto Prefecture at the end of

July is weighing on economic activity in the affected areas. Although moves toward
recovery can be seen, with many companies having already resumed operations,
the impact could become prolonged in the following scenarios:
The first is if damage to capital stock such as factories and roads has been severe.
Some companies have sustained damage to production facilities, making an early
resumption of operations difficult in some cases.
The second is if a downturn in tourism demand occurs. According to some media

reports, there has been an uptick in cancellations of accommodation reservations in
Kumamoto Prefecture and neighboring prefectures since the earthquake. After the
2016 Kumamoto earthquake, the decline in travel demand from Japanese visitors
was short lived, but lower demand from foreign tourists persisted for a long period.
Annualized real GDP growth was +1.1% quarter over quarter (QoQ) in Q2 2026

### Page 5

Monthly Report of Prospects for Japan's Economy August 2026
The Japan Research Institute, Limited
Exports and inbound demand remain solid despite weakness in some areas
◆ Goods exports are on the verge of picking up
Goods exports are treading water. A sharp rise in memory chip prices is among
factors pushing up export prices, resulting in record high levels of exports in value
terms recently. On the other hand, the increase in volume terms is limited. One

possible reason is that Japan's export competitiveness has declined in fields such as
semiconductors, where global demand is growing.
Looking ahead, exports are expected to pick up. Capital investment by chipmakers
is expanding worldwide, and this should lift exports of semiconductor manufacturing
equipment, an area in which Japan has strength. However, continued caution is
needed regarding the effects of prolonged turmoil in the Middle East. A global

slowdown due to elevated crude oil prices could weigh on exports.
◆ Reluctance to visit among Chinese travelers is weighing on demand for
travel to Japan
Inbound demand is currently soft. The Chinese government's advisory urging its
citizens to refrain from traveling to Japan is having an adverse impact. The number
of Chinese visitors to Japan has fallen to about half the level prior to the advisory.
Meanwhile, the number of tourists from countries other than China remains firm.

Inbound demand is expected to stay robust going forward. Continued increases in
tourists from countries other than China will remain a supporting factor. The
expectation that the yen will remain weak will also act to push up inbound
consumption. However, there are two downside risks going forward:
The first is that the Chinese government keeps the advisory in place for a
prolonged period. When China banned group travel to South Korea in 2017, tourist

numbers stayed low for two to three years.
The second is the impact of higher crude oil prices. Major airlines have embarked
on phased increases in fuel surcharges, which could weigh on inbound demand
during the summer.

### Page 6

Personal consumption is gradually recovering thanks to a favorable income environment
◆ Higher disposable incomes are supporting personal consumption
Personal consumption continues to gradually recover. While consumption of
non-durable goods remains weak, services consumption is firm, particularly in
amusement-related services. In addition, consumption of durable goods has
increased significantly. Temporary factors such as the abolition of the environmental

performance tax on automobiles (since April 2026) and the tightening of
energy-efficiency standards for air conditioners (effective April 2027) have also
contributed to this rise.
A favorable income environment can also be pointed to as a background factor
behind the recovery in personal consumption. YoY real disposable income growth has
generally been positive since mid-2024, and the pace of increase has recently
accelerated. Employee remuneration is rising, partly as a result of agreements made

during the shunto (spring wage negotiations), and net asset income is also increasing
at a faster clip as companies hike dividends in line with stock price rises.
◆ Stock price developments could be a source of concern for personal
consumption
Personal consumption is expected to continue its gradual recovery going forward. In
addition to the favorable income environment and government policy measures to
curb price increases, a recovery in consumer confidence driven by easing tensions in

the Middle East will also provide a tailwind. Recently, the Consumer Confidence Index
has been showing signs of bottoming out.
However, a reverse wealth effect stemming from falling stock prices is a potential
cause for concern. The Nikkei Stock Average, which reached a new high in June, has
been struggling to push higher since the start of July. Estimates suggest the wealth
effect is concentrated among elderly households, but in recent years working-age

households have also increased their holdings of equity-related assets, and the
positive wealth effect for them may have supported consumption to some extent. For
this reason, if stock prices were to fall significantly, there is a danger of downward
pressure on consumption across a wide range of households.

### Page 7

セン
To
◆ The unemployment rate has settled in the mid-2% range
While the employment situation is generally favorable, weakness can be seen in
some areas. The unemployment rate has remained in the mid-2% range since 2022.
The number of employed persons in non-manufacturing, where business conditions
are favorable, is holding firm, while in manufacturing the number remains at a level
below that seen before the COVID-19 pandemic.
In the background to the drop in manufacturing employment is a slump in

production activity. Manufacturing output has been shrinking due to the effects of
U.S. tariff policy, soaring raw material prices, and a decline in the international
competitiveness of Japanese firms. As a result, pressure to reduce headcounts has
been building. Even in non-manufacturing, some industries closely linked to
manufacturing, such as transportation and wholesale/retail, are also exhibiting
weakness in labor demand.
◆ The employment situation could remain fragile in the near term

Looking ahead at the employment situation in the short term, a sense of
stagnation may persist. The contraction in output in the manufacturing sector will
continue to weigh on labor demand. Although production activity has been brisker
recently, particularly in semiconductor-related industries, the employment-creation
capacity of such industries is small. The employment-inducement coefficient per unit
of new demand tends to be lower than in other sectors, meaning that the industries

lack the strength to serve as drivers of employment.
However, in the medium to long term, the unemployment rate is expected to see a
gradual decline. Demand for labor is projected to stay solid, especially in
face-to-face services such as medical/nursing care and accommodation and
eating/drinking services, but the number of people seeking employment from the
non-labor force population has declined significantly due to the expansion in elderly
and female workforce participation. With limited room for expansion in the labor

supply, the job market is expected to tighten further.

The government is curbing inflation
◆ Policy factors are pushing down the inflation rate
In June, core inflation (all items, less fresh food) was 1.6%, staying under 2%
for the fifth consecutive month. Food price inflation has slowed, and a major

reason for this is a fallback in rice prices, which soared last year. Gasoline
subsidies are also continuing to curtail energy price increases.
Inflation looks set to hover in the 2% range in the second half of 2026. Although
energy subsidies and other government measures will soften inflationary pressure
to some extent, with imported inflationary pressure building, prices are expected
to be pushed up as higher costs are passed through to selling prices. From

FY2027 onward, the reduction in the consumption tax rate for food items will
serve to keep prices down, and inflation is expected to drop into the 1% range.
◆ The Bank of Japan (BOJ) will keep its policy rate unchanged
At its July monetary policy meeting, the BOJ decided to keep its policy rate
unchanged.
Long-term interest rates remain high. This reflects concerns about inflation
resulting from the increase in oil prices and expectations of an expansion in fiscal

expenditure.
The BOJ is expected to step up the pace of interest rate hikes while closely
monitoring the impact of higher rates on the real economy and financial/capital
markets. The next rate hike is expected to be in September.
Long-term interest rates look likely to rise gradually, reflecting heightened
anticipation of rate hikes by the BOJ and increased fiscal spending by the
government. If jitters about a deterioration in fiscal discipline resulting from such

measures as energy subsidies or the consumption tax cut take hold, there is also
a risk that long-term interest rates will rise.

◆ Surging crude oil prices have become a source of upward pressure on
prices
With crude oil prices having spiked against the backdrop of the worsening situation
in the Middle East, corporate input costs have risen sharply, and pressure for price
pass-through is intensifying. Looking at the Final Demand-Intermediate Demand
price indexes, the margin of price increases at the upstream stage far exceeds that at
the downstream stage, and price pass-through pressure is also substantial at the

downstream stage.
According to estimates based on past data, the impact of rising oil prices involves
a time lag. Specifically, the push-up effect on the consumer price inflation rate
increases for around 10 months and continues to exert influence for a long period
thereafter.
◆ Price pass-through willingness is also stronger than before
Notably, food product manufacturers are planning to raise prices for a huge
number of items during the summer and beyond. According to Teikoku Databank, the

number of items slated for price increases as of the end of July, when it conducted its
latest survey, was higher than in the May and June surveys.
If government subsidies for electricity/gas bills and gasoline prices are scaled back
or ended, both energy and food prices will increase concurrently, and the consumer
price inflation rate may rise again.
Attention also needs to be paid to the possibility that price pass-through driven by
elevated oil prices will become prolonged. In the 2020s, the rate of price increases in

transactions between companies has been higher than in the 2010s, and firms'
medium-term outlooks for selling prices have also shifted upward. Events that trigger
cost increases, such as heightened geopolitical tensions, have been occurring
repeatedly, and companies may be more willing to pass through higher costs to
prices than they were before.

### Page 10

The economy is expected to continue to recover, but downside risks are present
◆ The economy is projected to stay on the recovery track
The Japanese economy should continue its gradual recovery. WTI crude oil
futures prices are expected to fall moderately going forward.
In the corporate sector, government support for investment is providing a tailwind,
and demand for investment for labor saving and digital transformation remains
robust. In addition, rising global demand for AI-related goods should support exports.

Supply chain disruptions resulting from the deterioration in the Middle East situation
will begin to ease, and corporate production activity is expected to gradually recover.
In the household sector, inflationary pressure is expected to strengthen from the
summer onward due to higher crude oil prices. In response, the government will
probably ease the inflationary pressure through measures such as energy subsidies.
While the pace of increase in personal consumption is likely to slow somewhat,

consumption is expected to remain resilient amid continued high wage growth. From
FY2027 onward, the reduction in the consumption tax rate for food items should also
support personal consumption.
◆ The Middle East situation and rising interest rates constitute risks
Real GDP growth is projected to remain solid, registering +0.8% in FY2026 and
+0.9% in FY2027.
However, if the situation in the Middle East worsens again or becomes prolonged,
there is a risk that downward pressure on the economy will intensify, mainly through

three channels: 1) weaker exports, 2) raw material supply constraints, and 3) rising
prices. In addition, if market concerns about the fiscal stance of the Takaichi
government grow, the yen could weaken further, and long-term interest rates could
rise, potentially forcing the scaling-back or scrapping of expansionary policies such
as investment support measures, energy subsidies, and lower consumption tax.
Monthly Report of Prospects for Japan's Economy August 2026

The Japan Research Institute, Limited- ８ -
